from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import hashlib
import json
import sys
from itertools import combinations_with_replacement
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Iterable, Sequence

import sympy as sp
from sympy.polys.matrices import DomainMatrix

from .program5_compression_export import (
    DEFAULT_SOURCE,
    _load_source,
    _monomial_label,
    _q,
    build_export,
)
from .program5_rank_six_second_order import (
    _flatten,
    _independent_rows_and_columns,
    _matrix_rank,
    _project_to_cokernel,
    _sympy_matrix,
)
from .program5_tangent_bridge import _extend_basis


def _digest(vector: sp.Matrix) -&gt; str:
    payload = &#91;str(sp.Rational(value)) for value in vector&#93;
    return hashlib.sha256(
        json.dumps(payload, separators=(",", ":")).encode("utf-8")
    ).hexdigest()


def _sparse_vector(
    vector: sp.Matrix,
    labels: Sequence&#91;str&#93;,
) -&gt; list&#91;dict&#91;str, Any&#93;&#93;:
    return &#91;
        {"coordinate": labels&#91;index&#93;, "coefficient": _q(vector&#91;index, 0&#93;)}
        for index in range(vector.rows)
        if vector&#91;index, 0&#93; != 0
    &#93;


def _rank_pair(matrix: sp.Matrix, rhs: sp.Matrix) -&gt; tuple&#91;int, int&#93;:
    return (
        DomainMatrix.from_Matrix(matrix, fmt="sparse").rank(),
        DomainMatrix.from_Matrix(matrix.row_join(rhs), fmt="sparse").rank(),
    )


def _deterministic_solution(
    matrix: sp.Matrix,
    rhs: sp.Matrix,
) -&gt; tuple&#91;bool, sp.Matrix | None, int, int, int&#93;:
    rank, augmented_rank = _rank_pair(matrix, rhs)
    if rank != augmented_rank:
        return False, None, rank, augmented_rank, matrix.cols - rank
    solution_tuple = next(iter(sp.linsolve((matrix, rhs))))
    free_symbols = sorted(
        set().union(*(entry.free_symbols for entry in solution_tuple)),
        key=str,
    )
    solution = sp.Matrix(
        &#91;
            sp.factor(entry.subs({symbol: 0 for symbol in free_symbols}))
            for entry in solution_tuple
        &#93;
    )
    if matrix * solution != rhs:
        raise AssertionError("deterministic tangent solution failed")
    return True, solution, rank, augmented_rank, len(free_symbols)


def analyze_line_scan(
    source_path: Path = DEFAULT_SOURCE,
    *,
    ratios: Iterable&#91;int | sp.Rational&#93; = (-3, -2, -1, 0, 1, 2, 3),
    verify_axes: bool = True,
) -&gt; dict&#91;str, Any&#93;:
    contract, exported = build_export(source_path)
    row_layer, rank_layer = contract&#91;"layers"&#93;
    operation_labels = list(row_layer&#91;"deformation_basis"&#93;)
    row_kernel = _sympy_matrix(row_layer&#91;"actions"&#93;&#91;0&#93;&#91;"action_matrix"&#93;)
    rank_kernel = _sympy_matrix(rank_layer&#91;"actions"&#93;&#91;0&#93;&#91;"action_matrix"&#93;)
    row_columns = &#91;row_kernel&#91;:, index&#93; for index in range(row_kernel.cols)&#93;
    rank_columns = &#91;rank_kernel&#91;:, index&#93; for index in range(rank_kernel.cols)&#93;
    adapted, complement = _extend_basis(row_columns, rank_columns)
    if len(adapted) != 22 or len(complement) != 2:
        raise AssertionError("the 20+2 tangent splitting changed")
    tangent_basis = adapted
    eta0, eta1 = complement
    theta_u = eta0 + row_columns&#91;4&#93;
    theta_v = eta1 + 4 * row_columns&#91;0&#93; - 24 * row_columns&#91;1&#93; - 4 * row_columns&#91;4&#93;

    source, _ = _load_source(source_path)
    V = tuple(source.V)
    Q = sp.Matrix(source.Q)
    C = sp.Matrix(source.C)
    weights = source.weights
    n = len(V)
    cubic_monomials = &#91;
        sp.prod(V&#91;index&#93; for index in indices)
        for indices in combinations_with_replacement(range(n), 3)
    &#93;
    quadratic_monomials = &#91;
        sp.prod(V&#91;index&#93; for index in indices)
        for indices in combinations_with_replacement(range(n), 2)
    &#93;

    def weight(monomial: sp.Expr) -&gt; int:
        exponents = sp.Poly(monomial, *V).monoms()&#91;0&#93;
        return sum(exponents&#91;index&#93; * weights&#91;V&#91;index&#93;&#93; for index in range(n))

    operation_basis: list&#91;tuple&#91;int, sp.Expr&#93;&#93; = &#91;&#93;
    for row, variable in enumerate(V):
        for monomial in quadratic_monomials:
            if weight(monomial) == weights&#91;variable&#93;:
                operation_basis.append((row, monomial))
    labels = &#91;
        f"e_{V&#91;row&#93;}*{_monomial_label(monomial)}"
        for row, monomial in operation_basis
    &#93;
    if len(operation_basis) != 115 or labels != operation_labels:
        raise AssertionError("operation basis changed")

    JQ = Q.jacobian(V)
    variation_matrices: list&#91;sp.Matrix&#93; = &#91;&#93;
    for operation_row, monomial in operation_basis:
        field = sp.zeros(n, 1)
        field&#91;operation_row, 0&#93; = monomial
        bracket = sp.Matrix(
            &#91;
                sp.expand(value)
                for value in JQ * field - field.jacobian(V) * Q
            &#93;
        )
        coefficient_matrix = sp.zeros(n, len(cubic_monomials))
        for row, expression in enumerate(bracket):
            polynomial = sp.Poly(expression, *V)
            for column, cubic_monomial in enumerate(cubic_monomials):
                coefficient_matrix&#91;row, column&#93; = polynomial.coeff_monomial(
                    cubic_monomial
                )
        variation_matrices.append(coefficient_matrix)

    def variation(vector: sp.Matrix) -&gt; sp.Matrix:
        result = sp.zeros(n, len(cubic_monomials))
        for coefficient, matrix in zip(vector, variation_matrices):
            if coefficient:
                result += coefficient * matrix
        return result

    p0 = sp.zeros(115, 1)
    p0_index = next(
        index
        for index, (row, monomial) in enumerate(operation_basis)
        if row == 3 and sp.expand(monomial - source.d**2) == 0
    )
    p0&#91;p0_index, 0&#93; = -1
    M0 = sp.zeros(n, len(cubic_monomials))
    for row, expression in enumerate(C):
        polynomial = sp.Poly(expression, *V)
        for column, monomial in enumerate(cubic_monomials):
            M0&#91;row, column&#93; = polynomial.coeff_monomial(monomial)
    M0 += variation(p0)
    if M0.rank() != 6:
        raise AssertionError("base cubic-coordinate matrix lost rank six")

    base_rows = &#91;0, 1, 2, 4, 5, 8&#93;
    zero_rows = &#91;3, 6, 7, 9, 10&#93;
    base_matrix = M0&#91;base_rows, :&#93;
    _, pivot_tuple = base_matrix.rref()
    pivot_columns = list(pivot_tuple)
    if len(pivot_columns) != 6:
        raise AssertionError("the fixed Schur minor lost rank six")
    nonpivot_columns = &#91;
        column for column in range(len(cubic_monomials))
        if column not in pivot_columns
    &#93;
    A0 = M0&#91;base_rows, pivot_columns&#93;
    B0 = M0&#91;base_rows, nonpivot_columns&#93;
    G0 = A0.inv()

    def blocks(vector: sp.Matrix) -&gt; dict&#91;str, sp.Matrix&#93;:
        matrix = variation(vector)
        return {
            "A": matrix&#91;base_rows, pivot_columns&#93;,
            "B": matrix&#91;base_rows, nonpivot_columns&#93;,
            "C": matrix&#91;zero_rows, pivot_columns&#93;,
            "D": matrix&#91;zero_rows, nonpivot_columns&#93;,
        }

    ambient_blocks = &#91;blocks(sp.eye(115)&#91;:, index&#93;) for index in range(115)&#93;
    L = sp.Matrix.hstack(
        *&#91;
            _flatten(block&#91;"D"&#93; - block&#91;"C"&#93; * G0 * B0)
            for block in ambient_blocks
        &#93;
    )
    if _matrix_rank(L) != 93:
        raise AssertionError("rank-six tangent map changed")
    for vector in tangent_basis:
        if L * vector != sp.zeros(L.rows, 1):
            raise AssertionError("adapted tangent basis left ker(L)")
    pivot_rows, pivot_operation_columns = _independent_rows_and_columns(L)
    minor = L&#91;pivot_rows, pivot_operation_columns&#93;
    minor_inverse = minor.inv()

    def project(forcing: sp.Matrix) -&gt; sp.Matrix:
        return _project_to_cokernel(
            forcing,
            L,
            pivot_rows=pivot_rows,
            pivot_columns=pivot_operation_columns,
            inverse_minor=minor_inverse,
        )

    def solve_image(forcing_matrix: sp.Matrix) -&gt; sp.Matrix:
        forcing = _flatten(forcing_matrix)
        residual = project(forcing)
        if any(residual):
            raise AssertionError("forcing is not in the tangent image")
        coefficients = minor_inverse * forcing&#91;list(pivot_rows), :&#93;
        correction = sp.zeros(115, 1)
        for index, column in enumerate(pivot_operation_columns):
            correction&#91;column, 0&#93; = coefficients&#91;index, 0&#93;
        if L * correction != forcing:
            raise AssertionError("deterministic image solution failed")
        return correction

    tangent_blocks = &#91;blocks(vector) for vector in tangent_basis&#93;

    def quadratic_forcing(theta_block: dict&#91;str, sp.Matrix&#93;) -&gt; sp.Matrix:
        G1 = -G0 * theta_block&#91;"A"&#93; * G0
        return (
            theta_block&#91;"C"&#93; * G0 * theta_block&#91;"B"&#93;
            + theta_block&#91;"C"&#93; * G1 * B0
        )

    def cubic_forcing(
        theta_block: dict&#91;str, sp.Matrix&#93;,
        p2_block: dict&#91;str, sp.Matrix&#93;,
    ) -&gt; sp.Matrix:
        G1 = -G0 * theta_block&#91;"A"&#93; * G0
        G2 = -G0 * (
            theta_block&#91;"A"&#93; * G1 + p2_block&#91;"A"&#93; * G0
        )
        return (
            theta_block&#91;"C"&#93; * G0 * p2_block&#91;"B"&#93;
            + theta_block&#91;"C"&#93; * G1 * theta_block&#91;"B"&#93;
            + theta_block&#91;"C"&#93; * G2 * B0
            + p2_block&#91;"C"&#93; * G0 * theta_block&#91;"B"&#93;
            + p2_block&#91;"C"&#93; * G1 * B0
        )

    def bilinear_effect(
        tangent_block: dict&#91;str, sp.Matrix&#93;,
        theta_block: dict&#91;str, sp.Matrix&#93;,
    ) -&gt; sp.Matrix:
        return (
            tangent_block&#91;"C"&#93; * G0 * theta_block&#91;"B"&#93;
            - tangent_block&#91;"C"&#93;
            * G0
            * theta_block&#91;"A"&#93;
            * G0
            * B0
            + theta_block&#91;"C"&#93; * G0 * tangent_block&#91;"B"&#93;
            - theta_block&#91;"C"&#93;
            * G0
            * tangent_block&#91;"A"&#93;
            * G0
            * B0
        )

    def obstruction_certificate(
        matrix: sp.Matrix,
        rhs: sp.Matrix,
        active_rows: Sequence&#91;int&#93;,
        *,
        order: int,
    ) -&gt; dict&#91;str, Any&#93;:
        witness = next(
            (
                vector
                for vector in matrix.T.nullspace()
                if (vector.T * rhs)&#91;0, 0&#93; != 0
            ),
            None,
        )
        if witness is None:
            raise AssertionError("rank mismatch has no left-null witness")
        first_nonzero = next(value for value in witness if value != 0)
        witness = sp.Matrix(&#91;sp.factor(value / first_nonzero) for value in witness&#93;)
        pairing = sp.factor((witness.T * rhs)&#91;0, 0&#93;)
        if witness.T * matrix != sp.zeros(1, matrix.cols) or pairing == 0:
            raise AssertionError("invalid line obstruction witness")
        coordinates = &#91;&#93;
        for local_row, coefficient in enumerate(witness):
            if coefficient == 0:
                continue
            residual_row = active_rows&#91;local_row&#93;
            schur_row, schur_column = divmod(
                residual_row,
                len(nonpivot_columns),
            )
            coordinates.append(
                {
                    "parameter_monomial": f"t^{order}",
                    "schur_row_variable": str(V&#91;zero_rows&#91;schur_row&#93;&#93;),
                    "schur_column_monomial": _monomial_label(
                        cubic_monomials&#91;nonpivot_columns&#91;schur_column&#93;&#93;
                    ),
                    "coefficient": _q(coefficient),
                }
            )
        return {
            "kind": "exact left-null witness",
            "pairing_with_rhs": _q(pairing),
            "nonzero_count": len(coordinates),
            "witness_sha256": _digest(witness),
            "coordinates": coordinates,
        }

    def analyze_direction(
        name: str,
        theta: sp.Matrix,
        *,
        ratio: int | sp.Rational | None,
        verify_effect: bool,
    ) -&gt; dict&#91;str, Any&#93;:
        theta_block = blocks(theta)
        H2 = quadratic_forcing(theta_block)
        second_residual = project(_flatten(H2))
        second_order_compatible = not any(second_residual)
        result: dict&#91;str, Any&#93; = {
            "name": name,
            "u_over_v": ratio if ratio is not None else "infinity",
            "first_order_nonzero_count": len(_sparse_vector(theta, labels)),
            "first_order_sha256": _digest(theta),
            "second_order_compatible": second_order_compatible,
        }
        if not second_order_compatible:
            result&#91;"first_obstructed_order"&#93; = 2
            result&#91;"second_order_obstruction_rank"&#93; = _matrix_rank(second_residual)
            return result

        P2 = solve_image(H2)
        P2_block = blocks(P2)
        H3 = cubic_forcing(theta_block, P2_block)
        cubic_residual = project(_flatten(H3))
        effect_columns = &#91;
            project(_flatten(bilinear_effect(tangent_block, theta_block)))
            for tangent_block in tangent_blocks
        &#93;
        if verify_effect:
            for tangent_index, tangent_block in enumerate(tangent_blocks):
                perturbed_block = {
                    key: P2_block&#91;key&#93; + tangent_block&#91;key&#93;
                    for key in P2_block
                }
                observed = project(
                    _flatten(cubic_forcing(theta_block, perturbed_block))
                ) - cubic_residual
                if observed != effect_columns&#91;tangent_index&#93;:
                    raise AssertionError(
                        "direct line-effect replay disagrees in tangent column "
                        f"{tangent_index} for {name}"
                    )

        active_rows = sorted(
            {
                row
                for row, value in enumerate(cubic_residual)
                if value
            }
            | {
                row
                for column in effect_columns
                for row, value in enumerate(column)
                if value
            }
        )
        effect_matrix = sp.Matrix(
            &#91;
                &#91;column&#91;row, 0&#93; for column in effect_columns&#93;
                for row in active_rows
            &#93;
        )
        rhs = sp.Matrix(&#91;-cubic_residual&#91;row, 0&#93; for row in active_rows&#93;)
        solvable, solution, rank, augmented_rank, free_dimension = (
            _deterministic_solution(effect_matrix, rhs)
        )
        result.update(
            {
                "quadratic_tangent_freedom_dimension": 22,
                "compressed_cubic_equation_count": len(active_rows),
                "cubic_effect_rank": rank,
                "cubic_augmented_rank": augmented_rank,
                "cubic_lift_solvable": solvable,
                "cubic_lift_solution_free_dimension": (
                    free_dimension if solvable else None
                ),
                "direct_effect_columns_verified": 22 if verify_effect else 0,
            }
        )
        if not solvable or solution is None:
            result&#91;"first_obstructed_order"&#93; = 3
            result&#91;"cubic_obstruction_certificate"&#93; = obstruction_certificate(
                effect_matrix,
                rhs,
                active_rows,
                order=3,
            )
            return result

        tangent_adjustment = sp.zeros(115, 1)
        coefficients = &#91;&#93;
        for index, tangent_vector in enumerate(tangent_basis):
            coefficient = solution&#91;index, 0&#93;
            if coefficient:
                tangent_adjustment += coefficient * tangent_vector
                coefficients.append(
                    {
                        "tangent_basis_index": index,
                        "coefficient": _q(coefficient),
                    }
                )
        adjusted_P2 = P2 + tangent_adjustment
        adjusted_H3 = cubic_forcing(theta_block, blocks(adjusted_P2))
        adjusted_residual = project(_flatten(adjusted_H3))
        if any(adjusted_residual):
            raise AssertionError("solved tangent adjustment left a cubic residual")
        P3 = solve_image(adjusted_H3)
        result.update(
            {
                "maximum_compatible_order": 3,
                "quadratic_tangent_adjustment": {
                    "coefficient_count": len(coefficients),
                    "coefficients": coefficients,
                    "nonzero_count": len(_sparse_vector(tangent_adjustment, labels)),
                    "sha256": _digest(tangent_adjustment),
                },
                "adjusted_quadratic_correction": {
                    "nonzero_count": len(_sparse_vector(adjusted_P2, labels)),
                    "sha256": _digest(adjusted_P2),
                },
                "cubic_correction": {
                    "nonzero_count": len(_sparse_vector(P3, labels)),
                    "sha256": _digest(P3),
                },
            }
        )
        return result

    finite_results = &#91;&#93;
    normalized_ratios = &#91;sp.Rational(value) for value in ratios&#93;
    for ratio in normalized_ratios:
        finite_results.append(
            analyze_direction(
                f"theta_v + ({ratio}) theta_u",
                theta_v + ratio * theta_u,
                ratio=ratio,
                verify_effect=verify_axes and ratio == 0,
            )
        )
    infinity_result = analyze_direction(
        "theta_u",
        theta_u,
        ratio=None,
        verify_effect=verify_axes,
    )

    return {
        "schema_version": 1,
        "name": "Program 5 projective rank-six line scan through cubic order",
        "source_file": str(source_path),
        "source_sha256": exported&#91;"summary"&#93;&#91;"source_sha256"&#93;,
        "ambient_operation_dimension": 115,
        "rank_six_tangent_dimension": 22,
        "selected_plane": {
            "theta_u": "eta_0 + xi_4",
            "theta_v": "eta_1 + 4*xi_0 - 24*xi_1 - 4*xi_4",
        },
        "finite_ratio_results": finite_results,
        "infinity_result": infinity_result,
        "interpretation_boundary": (
            "Each entry decides formal compatibility through cubic parameter "
            "order for one selected projective tangent direction after all "
            "quadratic tangent freedom is allowed. The finite sample is not a "
            "classification of every rational or algebraic ratio."
        ),
    }


def main(argv: Sequence&#91;str&#93; | None = None) -&gt; int:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument("--source", type=Path, default=DEFAULT_SOURCE)
    parser.add_argument("--output", type=Path)
    parser.add_argument(
        "--ratios",
        default="-3,-2,-1,0,1,2,3",
        help="comma-separated rational values u/v",
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        "--skip-direct-effect-checks",
        action="store_true",
    )
    args = parser.parse_args(argv)
    try:
        ratios = &#91;sp.Rational(value.strip()) for value in args.ratios.split(",")&#93;
        result = analyze_line_scan(
            args.source,
            ratios=ratios,
            verify_axes=not args.skip_direct_effect_checks,
        )
    except (OSError, ValueError, TypeError, AssertionError) as exc:
        print(f"program5_rank_six_line_scan: {exc}", file=sys.stderr)
        return 2
    rendered = json.dumps(result, indent=2, sort_keys=True) + "\n"
    if args.output:
        args.output.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        args.output.write_text(rendered, encoding="utf-8")
    else:
        sys.stdout.write(rendered)
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())

## `research-tools/filtered_operation_complex/adapters/program5_rank_six_schur_model.py`

<pre><code class="language-python">
"""Shared exact Schur-complement model for the Program 5 rank-six locus.

This module reconstructs the public 115-dimensional quadratic source-field
space, the rank-six base coefficient matrix, its fixed invertible 6-by-6
minor, the rank-93 tangent operator, and the adapted 20+2 tangent splitting.
It is intentionally internal research infrastructure for the higher Kuranishi
audits; it does not add a new mathematical claim by itself.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from itertools import combinations_with_replacement
from pathlib import Path

import sympy as sp

from .program5_compression_export import (
    DEFAULT_SOURCE,
    _load_source,
    _monomial_label,
    build_export,
)
from .program5_rank_six_second_order import (
    _flatten,
    _independent_rows_and_columns,
    _matrix_rank,
    _sympy_matrix,
)
from .program5_tangent_bridge import _extend_basis


@dataclass
class Program5RankSixSchurModel:
    source_path: Path
    source_sha256: str
    V: tuple&#91;sp.Symbol, ...&#93;
    labels: list&#91;str&#93;
    cubic_monomials: list&#91;sp.Expr&#93;
    variation_matrices: list&#91;sp.Matrix&#93;
    base_rows: list&#91;int&#93;
    zero_rows: list&#91;int&#93;
    pivot_columns: list&#91;int&#93;
    nonpivot_columns: list&#91;int&#93;
    G0: sp.Matrix
    B0: sp.Matrix
    L: sp.Matrix
    pivot_rows: tuple&#91;int, ...&#93;
    pivot_operation_columns: tuple&#91;int, ...&#93;
    minor_inverse: sp.Matrix
    tangent_basis: list&#91;sp.Matrix&#93;
    row_basis: list&#91;sp.Matrix&#93;
    eta0: sp.Matrix
    eta1: sp.Matrix
    theta_u: sp.Matrix
    theta_v: sp.Matrix

    @property
    def ambient_operation_dimension(self) -&gt; int:
        return len(self.labels)

    @property
    def residual_dimension(self) -&gt; int:
        return self.L.rows

    def variation(self, vector: sp.Matrix) -&gt; sp.Matrix:
        result = sp.zeros(len(self.V), len(self.cubic_monomials))
        for coefficient, matrix in zip(vector, self.variation_matrices):
            if coefficient:
                result += coefficient * matrix
        return result

    def blocks(self, vector: sp.Matrix) -&gt; dict&#91;str, sp.Matrix&#93;:
        matrix = self.variation(vector)
        return {
            "A": matrix&#91;self.base_rows, self.pivot_columns&#93;,
            "B": matrix&#91;self.base_rows, self.nonpivot_columns&#93;,
            "C": matrix&#91;self.zero_rows, self.pivot_columns&#93;,
            "D": matrix&#91;self.zero_rows, self.nonpivot_columns&#93;,
        }

    def project(self, forcing: sp.Matrix) -&gt; sp.Matrix:
        selected_rhs = forcing&#91;list(self.pivot_rows), :&#93;
        coefficients = self.minor_inverse * selected_rhs
        residual = (
            forcing
            - self.L&#91;:, list(self.pivot_operation_columns)&#93; * coefficients
        ).applyfunc(lambda value: sp.factor(sp.cancel(value)))
        if any(residual&#91;row, 0&#93; != 0 for row in self.pivot_rows):
            raise AssertionError("cokernel projection did not kill pivot rows")
        return residual

    def solve_image(self, forcing_matrix: sp.Matrix) -&gt; sp.Matrix:
        forcing = _flatten(forcing_matrix)
        residual = self.project(forcing)
        if any(value != 0 for value in residual):
            raise AssertionError("forcing is not in the tangent image")
        coefficients = self.minor_inverse * forcing&#91;list(self.pivot_rows), :&#93;
        correction = sp.zeros(self.ambient_operation_dimension, 1)
        for index, column in enumerate(self.pivot_operation_columns):
            correction&#91;column, 0&#93; = sp.factor(sp.cancel(coefficients&#91;index, 0&#93;))
        error = (self.L * correction - forcing).applyfunc(
            lambda value: sp.factor(sp.cancel(value))
        )
        if any(value != 0 for value in error):
            raise AssertionError("deterministic image solution failed")
        return correction

    def quadratic_forcing(
        self,
        theta_block: dict&#91;str, sp.Matrix&#93;,
    ) -&gt; sp.Matrix:
        G1 = -self.G0 * theta_block&#91;"A"&#93; * self.G0
        return (
            theta_block&#91;"C"&#93; * self.G0 * theta_block&#91;"B"&#93;
            + theta_block&#91;"C"&#93; * G1 * self.B0
        )

    def cubic_forcing(
        self,
        theta_block: dict&#91;str, sp.Matrix&#93;,
        p2_block: dict&#91;str, sp.Matrix&#93;,
    ) -&gt; sp.Matrix:
        G1 = -self.G0 * theta_block&#91;"A"&#93; * self.G0
        G2 = -self.G0 * (
            theta_block&#91;"A"&#93; * G1 + p2_block&#91;"A"&#93; * self.G0
        )
        return (
            theta_block&#91;"C"&#93; * self.G0 * p2_block&#91;"B"&#93;
            + theta_block&#91;"C"&#93; * G1 * theta_block&#91;"B"&#93;
            + theta_block&#91;"C"&#93; * G2 * self.B0
            + p2_block&#91;"C"&#93; * self.G0 * theta_block&#91;"B"&#93;
            + p2_block&#91;"C"&#93; * G1 * self.B0
        )

    def bilinear_effect(
        self,
        tangent_block: dict&#91;str, sp.Matrix&#93;,
        theta_block: dict&#91;str, sp.Matrix&#93;,
    ) -&gt; sp.Matrix:
        return (
            tangent_block&#91;"C"&#93; * self.G0 * theta_block&#91;"B"&#93;
            - tangent_block&#91;"C"&#93;
            * self.G0
            * theta_block&#91;"A"&#93;
            * self.G0
            * self.B0
            + theta_block&#91;"C"&#93; * self.G0 * tangent_block&#91;"B"&#93;
            - theta_block&#91;"C"&#93;
            * self.G0
            * tangent_block&#91;"A"&#93;
            * self.G0
            * self.B0
        )

    def residual_coordinate(self, residual_row: int) -&gt; dict&#91;str, str&#93;:
        schur_row, schur_column = divmod(
            residual_row,
            len(self.nonpivot_columns),
        )
        return {
            "schur_row_variable": str(self.V&#91;self.zero_rows&#91;schur_row&#93;&#93;),
            "schur_column_monomial": _monomial_label(
                self.cubic_monomials&#91;
                    self.nonpivot_columns&#91;schur_column&#93;
                &#93;
            ),
        }


def build_schur_model(
    source_path: Path = DEFAULT_SOURCE,
) -&gt; Program5RankSixSchurModel:
    contract, exported = build_export(source_path)
    row_layer, rank_layer = contract&#91;"layers"&#93;
    operation_labels = list(row_layer&#91;"deformation_basis"&#93;)
    row_kernel = _sympy_matrix(row_layer&#91;"actions"&#93;&#91;0&#93;&#91;"action_matrix"&#93;)
    rank_kernel = _sympy_matrix(rank_layer&#91;"actions"&#93;&#91;0&#93;&#91;"action_matrix"&#93;)
    row_columns = &#91;row_kernel&#91;:, index&#93; for index in range(row_kernel.cols)&#93;
    rank_columns = &#91;rank_kernel&#91;:, index&#93; for index in range(rank_kernel.cols)&#93;
    adapted, complement = _extend_basis(row_columns, rank_columns)
    if len(adapted) != 22 or len(complement) != 2:
        raise AssertionError("the 20+2 tangent splitting changed")
    eta0, eta1 = complement
    theta_u = eta0 + row_columns&#91;4&#93;
    theta_v = eta1 + 4 * row_columns&#91;0&#93; - 24 * row_columns&#91;1&#93; - 4 * row_columns&#91;4&#93;

    source, _ = _load_source(source_path)
    V = tuple(source.V)
    Q = sp.Matrix(source.Q)
    C = sp.Matrix(source.C)
    weights = source.weights
    n = len(V)
    cubic_monomials = &#91;
        sp.prod(V&#91;index&#93; for index in indices)
        for indices in combinations_with_replacement(range(n), 3)
    &#93;
    quadratic_monomials = &#91;
        sp.prod(V&#91;index&#93; for index in indices)
        for indices in combinations_with_replacement(range(n), 2)
    &#93;

    def weight(monomial: sp.Expr) -&gt; int:
        exponents = sp.Poly(monomial, *V).monoms()&#91;0&#93;
        return sum(exponents&#91;index&#93; * weights&#91;V&#91;index&#93;&#93; for index in range(n))

    operation_basis: list&#91;tuple&#91;int, sp.Expr&#93;&#93; = &#91;&#93;
    for row, variable in enumerate(V):
        for monomial in quadratic_monomials:
            if weight(monomial) == weights&#91;variable&#93;:
                operation_basis.append((row, monomial))
    labels = &#91;
        f"e_{V&#91;row&#93;}*{_monomial_label(monomial)}"
        for row, monomial in operation_basis
    &#93;
    if len(operation_basis) != 115 or labels != operation_labels:
        raise AssertionError("operation basis changed")

    JQ = Q.jacobian(V)
    variation_matrices: list&#91;sp.Matrix&#93; = &#91;&#93;
    for operation_row, monomial in operation_basis:
        field = sp.zeros(n, 1)
        field&#91;operation_row, 0&#93; = monomial
        bracket = sp.Matrix(
            &#91;
                sp.expand(value)
                for value in JQ * field - field.jacobian(V) * Q
            &#93;
        )
        coefficient_matrix = sp.zeros(n, len(cubic_monomials))
        for row, expression in enumerate(bracket):
            polynomial = sp.Poly(expression, *V)
            for column, cubic_monomial in enumerate(cubic_monomials):
                coefficient_matrix&#91;row, column&#93; = polynomial.coeff_monomial(
                    cubic_monomial
                )
        variation_matrices.append(coefficient_matrix)

    def variation(vector: sp.Matrix) -&gt; sp.Matrix:
        result = sp.zeros(n, len(cubic_monomials))
        for coefficient, matrix in zip(vector, variation_matrices):
            if coefficient:
                result += coefficient * matrix
        return result

    p0 = sp.zeros(115, 1)
    p0_index = next(
        index
        for index, (row, monomial) in enumerate(operation_basis)
        if row == 3 and sp.expand(monomial - source.d**2) == 0
    )
    p0&#91;p0_index, 0&#93; = -1
    M0 = sp.zeros(n, len(cubic_monomials))
    for row, expression in enumerate(C):
        polynomial = sp.Poly(expression, *V)
        for column, monomial in enumerate(cubic_monomials):
            M0&#91;row, column&#93; = polynomial.coeff_monomial(monomial)
    M0 += variation(p0)
    if M0.rank() != 6:
        raise AssertionError("base cubic-coordinate matrix lost rank six")

    base_rows = &#91;0, 1, 2, 4, 5, 8&#93;
    zero_rows = &#91;3, 6, 7, 9, 10&#93;
    base_matrix = M0&#91;base_rows, :&#93;
    _, pivot_tuple = base_matrix.rref()
    pivot_columns = list(pivot_tuple)
    if len(pivot_columns) != 6:
        raise AssertionError("the fixed Schur minor lost rank six")
    nonpivot_columns = &#91;
        column for column in range(len(cubic_monomials))
        if column not in pivot_columns
    &#93;
    A0 = M0&#91;base_rows, pivot_columns&#93;
    B0 = M0&#91;base_rows, nonpivot_columns&#93;
    G0 = A0.inv()

    def blocks(vector: sp.Matrix) -&gt; dict&#91;str, sp.Matrix&#93;:
        matrix = variation(vector)
        return {
            "A": matrix&#91;base_rows, pivot_columns&#93;,
            "B": matrix&#91;base_rows, nonpivot_columns&#93;,
            "C": matrix&#91;zero_rows, pivot_columns&#93;,
            "D": matrix&#91;zero_rows, nonpivot_columns&#93;,
        }

    ambient_blocks = &#91;blocks(sp.eye(115)&#91;:, index&#93;) for index in range(115)&#93;
    L = sp.Matrix.hstack(
        *&#91;
            _flatten(block&#91;"D"&#93; - block&#91;"C"&#93; * G0 * B0)
            for block in ambient_blocks
        &#93;
    )
    if _matrix_rank(L) != 93:
        raise AssertionError("rank-six tangent map changed")
    for vector in adapted:
        if L * vector != sp.zeros(L.rows, 1):
            raise AssertionError("adapted tangent basis left ker(L)")
    pivot_rows, pivot_operation_columns = _independent_rows_and_columns(L)
    minor = L&#91;pivot_rows, pivot_operation_columns&#93;
    minor_inverse = minor.inv()

    return Program5RankSixSchurModel(
        source_path=source_path,
        source_sha256=exported&#91;"summary"&#93;&#91;"source_sha256"&#93;,
        V=V,
        labels=labels,
        cubic_monomials=cubic_monomials,
        variation_matrices=variation_matrices,
        base_rows=base_rows,
        zero_rows=zero_rows,
        pivot_columns=pivot_columns,
        nonpivot_columns=nonpivot_columns,
        G0=G0,
        B0=B0,
        L=L,
        pivot_rows=tuple(pivot_rows),
        pivot_operation_columns=tuple(pivot_operation_columns),
        minor_inverse=minor_inverse,
        tangent_basis=adapted,
        row_basis=row_columns,
        eta0=eta0,
        eta1=eta1,
        theta_u=theta_u,
        theta_v=theta_v,
    )
</code></pre>

## `research-tools/filtered_operation_complex/adapters/program5_rank_six_second_order.py`

<pre><code class="language-python">
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Second-order Kuranishi test for the Program 5 rank-six condition.

Let ``M(P)`` be the 11-by-286 cubic-coordinate coefficient matrix of

    C + &#91;Q,P&#93;

inside the 115-dimensional space of weight-preserving quadratic source
fields.  At ``P0=-d^2 e_a``, ``M(P0)`` has rank six.  Choose the six nonzero
base rows and a nonzero six-by-six pivot minor.  In this chart the rank-at-most
six equations are the Schur complement

    F(P) = D(P) - C(P) A(P)^(-1) B(P) = 0.

The first derivative has a 22-dimensional kernel.  This script computes the
quadratic forcing

    L(zeta) + Q2(xi) = 0

for an arbitrary tangent vector ``xi`` in that kernel, projects ``Q2`` exactly
to the cokernel of ``L``, and reports the resulting homogeneous quadratic
Kuranishi equations.

The calculation decides second-order formal compatibility only.  It is not an
all-order algebraization theorem and does not quotient by every source,
target, or stable-presentation operation.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json
import sys
from itertools import combinations_with_replacement
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Sequence

import sympy as sp
from sympy.polys.matrices import DomainMatrix

from .program5_compression_export import (
    DEFAULT_SOURCE,
    _load_source,
    _monomial_label,
    _q,
    build_export,
)
from .program5_tangent_bridge import _extend_basis


def _sympy_matrix(values: Sequence&#91;Sequence&#91;Any&#93;&#93;) -&gt; sp.Matrix:
    return sp.Matrix(
        &#91;&#91;sp.Rational(value) for value in row&#93; for row in values&#93;
    )


def _polynomial_terms(
    expression: sp.Expr,
    variables: Sequence&#91;sp.Symbol&#93;,
) -&gt; list&#91;dict&#91;str, Any&#93;&#93;:
    polynomial = sp.Poly(sp.expand(expression), *variables, domain=sp.QQ)
    result = &#91;&#93;
    for exponents, coefficient in polynomial.terms():
        parts = &#91;&#93;
        for variable, exponent in zip(variables, exponents):
            if not exponent:
                continue
            parts.append(
                str(variable) if exponent == 1 else f"{variable}^{exponent}"
            )
        result.append(
            {
                "exponents": list(exponents),
                "monomial": "*".join(parts) or "1",
                "coefficient": _q(coefficient),
            }
        )
    return result


def _matrix_rank(matrix: sp.MatrixBase) -&gt; int:
    return DomainMatrix.from_Matrix(matrix, fmt="sparse").rank()


def _flatten(matrix: sp.MatrixBase) -&gt; sp.Matrix:
    return sp.Matrix(
        &#91;matrix&#91;row, column&#93; for row in range(matrix.rows) for column in range(matrix.cols)&#93;
    )


def _independent_rows_and_columns(matrix: sp.Matrix) -&gt; tuple&#91;list&#91;int&#93;, list&#91;int&#93;&#93;:
    column_pivots = list(
        DomainMatrix.from_Matrix(matrix, fmt="sparse").rref()&#91;1&#93;
    )
    row_pivots = list(
        DomainMatrix.from_Matrix(matrix.T, fmt="sparse").rref()&#91;1&#93;
    )
    if len(column_pivots) != len(row_pivots):
        raise AssertionError("row and column ranks disagree")
    return row_pivots, column_pivots


def _project_to_cokernel(
    vector: sp.Matrix,
    image_matrix: sp.Matrix,
    *,
    pivot_rows: Sequence&#91;int&#93;,
    pivot_columns: Sequence&#91;int&#93;,
    inverse_minor: sp.Matrix,
) -&gt; sp.Matrix:
    selected_rhs = vector&#91;list(pivot_rows), :&#93;
    coefficients = inverse_minor * selected_rhs
    residual = vector - image_matrix&#91;:, list(pivot_columns)&#93; * coefficients
    if any(residual&#91;row, 0&#93; for row in pivot_rows):
        raise AssertionError("cokernel projection did not kill pivot rows")
    return sp.Matrix(&#91;sp.factor(value) for value in residual&#93;)


def _rank_pair(matrix: sp.Matrix, rhs: sp.Matrix) -&gt; tuple&#91;int, int&#93;:
    return _matrix_rank(matrix), _matrix_rank(matrix.row_join(rhs))


def analyze_second_order(source_path: Path = DEFAULT_SOURCE) -&gt; dict&#91;str, Any&#93;:
    contract, exported = build_export(source_path)
    row_layer, rank_layer = contract&#91;"layers"&#93;
    operation_labels = list(row_layer&#91;"deformation_basis"&#93;)
    row_kernel = _sympy_matrix(row_layer&#91;"actions"&#93;&#91;0&#93;&#91;"action_matrix"&#93;)
    rank_kernel = _sympy_matrix(rank_layer&#91;"actions"&#93;&#91;0&#93;&#91;"action_matrix"&#93;)
    row_columns = &#91;row_kernel&#91;:, index&#93; for index in range(row_kernel.cols)&#93;
    rank_columns = &#91;rank_kernel&#91;:, index&#93; for index in range(rank_kernel.cols)&#93;
    adapted, complement = _extend_basis(row_columns, rank_columns)
    if len(adapted) != 22 or len(complement) != 2:
        raise AssertionError("the 20+2 adapted tangent splitting changed")
    tangent_basis = adapted

    source, _ = _load_source(source_path)
    V = tuple(source.V)
    Q = sp.Matrix(source.Q)
    C = sp.Matrix(source.C)
    weights = source.weights
    n = len(V)
    cubic_monomials = &#91;
        sp.prod(V&#91;index&#93; for index in indices)
        for indices in combinations_with_replacement(range(n), 3)
    &#93;
    quadratic_monomials = &#91;
        sp.prod(V&#91;index&#93; for index in indices)
        for indices in combinations_with_replacement(range(n), 2)
    &#93;

    def weight(monomial: sp.Expr) -&gt; int:
        exponents = sp.Poly(monomial, *V).monoms()&#91;0&#93;
        return sum(exponents&#91;index&#93; * weights&#91;V&#91;index&#93;&#93; for index in range(n))

    operation_basis: list&#91;tuple&#91;int, sp.Expr&#93;&#93; = &#91;&#93;
    for row, variable in enumerate(V):
        for monomial in quadratic_monomials:
            if weight(monomial) == weights&#91;variable&#93;:
                operation_basis.append((row, monomial))
    if len(operation_basis) != 115:
        raise AssertionError("the operation space no longer has dimension 115")
    labels = &#91;
        f"e_{V&#91;row&#93;}*{_monomial_label(monomial)}"
        for row, monomial in operation_basis
    &#93;
    if labels != operation_labels:
        raise AssertionError("operation basis order changed")

    JQ = Q.jacobian(V)
    variation_matrices: list&#91;sp.Matrix&#93; = &#91;&#93;
    for operation_row, monomial in operation_basis:
        field = sp.zeros(n, 1)
        field&#91;operation_row, 0&#93; = monomial
        bracket = sp.Matrix(
            &#91;
                sp.expand(value)
                for value in JQ * field - field.jacobian(V) * Q
            &#93;
        )
        coefficient_matrix = sp.zeros(n, len(cubic_monomials))
        for row, expression in enumerate(bracket):
            polynomial = sp.Poly(expression, *V)
            for column, cubic_monomial in enumerate(cubic_monomials):
                coefficient_matrix&#91;row, column&#93; = polynomial.coeff_monomial(
                    cubic_monomial
                )
        variation_matrices.append(coefficient_matrix)

    p0 = sp.zeros(115, 1)
    p0_index = next(
        index
        for index, (row, monomial) in enumerate(operation_basis)
        if row == 3 and sp.expand(monomial - source.d**2) == 0
    )
    p0&#91;p0_index, 0&#93; = -1
    M0 = sp.zeros(n, len(cubic_monomials))
    for row, expression in enumerate(C):
        polynomial = sp.Poly(expression, *V)
        for column, monomial in enumerate(cubic_monomials):
            M0&#91;row, column&#93; = polynomial.coeff_monomial(monomial)
    for coefficient, variation in zip(p0, variation_matrices):
        if coefficient:
            M0 += coefficient * variation
    if M0.rank() != 6:
        raise AssertionError("base cubic-coordinate matrix lost rank six")

    base_rows = &#91;0, 1, 2, 4, 5, 8&#93;
    zero_rows = &#91;3, 6, 7, 9, 10&#93;
    base_matrix = M0&#91;base_rows, :&#93;
    _, pivot_columns_tuple = base_matrix.rref()
    pivot_columns = list(pivot_columns_tuple)
    if len(pivot_columns) != 6:
        raise AssertionError("base row matrix lost rank six")
    nonpivot_columns = &#91;
        column for column in range(len(cubic_monomials))
        if column not in pivot_columns
    &#93;
    A0 = M0&#91;base_rows, pivot_columns&#93;
    B0 = M0&#91;base_rows, nonpivot_columns&#93;
    A0_inverse = A0.inv()

    def variation(vector: sp.Matrix) -&gt; sp.Matrix:
        result = sp.zeros(n, len(cubic_monomials))
        for coefficient, matrix in zip(vector, variation_matrices):
            if coefficient:
                result += coefficient * matrix
        return result

    tangent_variations = &#91;variation(vector) for vector in tangent_basis&#93;
    blocks = &#91;&#93;
    for matrix in tangent_variations:
        blocks.append(
            {
                "a": matrix&#91;base_rows, pivot_columns&#93;,
                "b": matrix&#91;base_rows, nonpivot_columns&#93;,
                "c": matrix&#91;zero_rows, pivot_columns&#93;,
                "d": matrix&#91;zero_rows, nonpivot_columns&#93;,
            }
        )

    ambient_tangent_columns = &#91;&#93;
    ambient_blocks = &#91;&#93;
    for matrix in variation_matrices:
        block = {
            "a": matrix&#91;base_rows, pivot_columns&#93;,
            "b": matrix&#91;base_rows, nonpivot_columns&#93;,
            "c": matrix&#91;zero_rows, pivot_columns&#93;,
            "d": matrix&#91;zero_rows, nonpivot_columns&#93;,
        }
        ambient_blocks.append(block)
        linear = block&#91;"d"&#93; - block&#91;"c"&#93; * A0_inverse * B0
        ambient_tangent_columns.append(_flatten(linear))
    L = sp.Matrix.hstack(*ambient_tangent_columns)
    if _matrix_rank(L) != 93:
        raise AssertionError("rank-six tangent map no longer has rank 93")
    for vector in tangent_basis:
        if L * vector != sp.zeros(L.rows, 1):
            raise AssertionError("adapted vector left the rank-six tangent kernel")

    pivot_rows, pivot_operation_columns = _independent_rows_and_columns(L)
    if len(pivot_rows) != 93:
        raise AssertionError("failed to find a rank-93 tangent minor")
    tangent_minor = L&#91;pivot_rows, pivot_operation_columns&#93;
    if tangent_minor.det() == 0:
        raise AssertionError("selected tangent minor is singular")
    tangent_minor_inverse = tangent_minor.inv()

    parameter_names = &#91;f"s{index}" for index in range(20)&#93; + &#91;"u", "v"&#93;
    parameters = sp.symbols(" ".join(parameter_names))
    monomial_pairs = list(combinations_with_replacement(range(22), 2))
    monomial_labels = &#91;
        (
            parameter_names&#91;left&#93; + "^2"
            if left == right
            else parameter_names&#91;left&#93; + "*" + parameter_names&#91;right&#93;
        )
        for left, right in monomial_pairs
    &#93;

    obstruction_columns: list&#91;sp.Matrix&#93; = &#91;&#93;
    nonzero_monomials: list&#91;str&#93; = &#91;&#93;
    raw_quadratic_columns: list&#91;sp.Matrix&#93; = &#91;&#93;
    for (left, right), label in zip(monomial_pairs, monomial_labels):
        left_block = blocks&#91;left&#93;
        right_block = blocks&#91;right&#93;

        def ordered(first: dict&#91;str, sp.Matrix&#93;, second: dict&#91;str, sp.Matrix&#93;) -&gt; sp.Matrix:
            return (
                -first&#91;"c"&#93; * A0_inverse * second&#91;"b"&#93;
                + first&#91;"c"&#93;
                * A0_inverse
                * second&#91;"a"&#93;
                * A0_inverse
                * B0
            )

        quadratic = ordered(left_block, right_block)
        if left != right:
            quadratic += ordered(right_block, left_block)
        raw_vector = _flatten(quadratic)
        residual = _project_to_cokernel(
            raw_vector,
            L,
            pivot_rows=pivot_rows,
            pivot_columns=pivot_operation_columns,
            inverse_minor=tangent_minor_inverse,
        )
        raw_quadratic_columns.append(raw_vector)
        obstruction_columns.append(residual)
        if any(residual):
            nonzero_monomials.append(label)

    obstruction_matrix = sp.Matrix.hstack(*obstruction_columns)
    obstruction_rank = _matrix_rank(obstruction_matrix)
    obstruction_row_pivots = list(
        DomainMatrix.from_Matrix(obstruction_matrix.T, fmt="sparse").rref()&#91;1&#93;
    )
    if len(obstruction_row_pivots) != obstruction_rank:
        raise AssertionError("failed to select independent Kuranishi equations")

    equations = &#91;&#93;
    equation_matrix = obstruction_matrix&#91;obstruction_row_pivots, :&#93;
    for equation_index in range(equation_matrix.rows):
        expression = sp.expand(
            sum(
                equation_matrix&#91;equation_index, monomial_index&#93;
                * parameters&#91;left&#93;
                * parameters&#91;right&#93;
                for monomial_index, (left, right) in enumerate(monomial_pairs)
            )
        )
        equations.append(expression)

    pure_row_equations = &#91;
        expression.subs({parameters&#91;20&#93;: 0, parameters&#91;21&#93;: 0})
        for expression in equations
    &#93;
    if any(sp.expand(expression) != 0 for expression in pure_row_equations):
        raise AssertionError("row-zero tangent directions acquired a quadratic obstruction")

    u, v = parameters&#91;20&#93;, parameters&#91;21&#93;
    A = sp.zeros(len(equations), 20)
    B = sp.zeros(len(equations), 20)
    c_u2 = sp.zeros(len(equations), 1)
    c_uv = sp.zeros(len(equations), 1)
    c_v2 = sp.zeros(len(equations), 1)
    for row, expression in enumerate(equations):
        polynomial = sp.Poly(expression, *parameters, domain=sp.QQ)
        for index in range(20):
            A&#91;row, index&#93; = polynomial.coeff_monomial(parameters&#91;index&#93; * u)
            B&#91;row, index&#93; = polynomial.coeff_monomial(parameters&#91;index&#93; * v)
        c_u2&#91;row, 0&#93; = polynomial.coeff_monomial(u**2)
        c_uv&#91;row, 0&#93; = polynomial.coeff_monomial(u * v)
        c_v2&#91;row, 0&#93; = polynomial.coeff_monomial(v**2)

    axis_u_ranks = _rank_pair(A, -c_u2)
    axis_v_ranks = _rank_pair(B, -c_v2)

    t = sp.symbols("t")
    generic_matrix = t * A + B
    generic_rhs = -(t**2 * c_u2 + t * c_uv + c_v2)
    generic_rank = DomainMatrix.from_Matrix(
        generic_matrix,
        fmt="sparse",
    ).to_field().rank()
    generic_augmented_rank = DomainMatrix.from_Matrix(
        generic_matrix.row_join(generic_rhs),
        fmt="sparse",
    ).to_field().rank()

    sample_ratios = &#91;&#93;
    for ratio in (-3, -2, -1, 0, 1, 2, 3):
        matrix = ratio * A + B
        rhs = -(ratio**2 * c_u2 + ratio * c_uv + c_v2)
        ranks = _rank_pair(matrix, rhs)
        sample_ratios.append(
            {
                "u_over_v": ratio,
                "matrix_rank": ranks&#91;0&#93;,
                "augmented_rank": ranks&#91;1&#93;,
                "second_order_solvable": ranks&#91;0&#93; == ranks&#91;1&#93;,
            }
        )

    compact_equations = &#91;&#93;
    for index, expression in enumerate(equations):
        compact_equations.append(
            {
                "index": index,
                "source_target_row": obstruction_row_pivots&#91;index&#93;,
                "terms": _polynomial_terms(expression, parameters),
            }
        )

    return {
        "schema_version": 1,
        "name": "Program 5 rank-six second-order Kuranishi audit",
        "source_file": str(source_path),
        "source_sha256": exported&#91;"summary"&#93;&#91;"source_sha256"&#93;,
        "ambient_operation_dimension": 115,
        "tangent_map_rank": 93,
        "tangent_dimension": 22,
        "row_zero_subspace_dimension": 20,
        "tangent_excess_dimension": 2,
        "local_rank_chart": {
            "base_rows": &#91;str(V&#91;index&#93;) for index in base_rows&#93;,
            "zero_rows": &#91;str(V&#91;index&#93;) for index in zero_rows&#93;,
            "pivot_cubic_monomials": &#91;
                _monomial_label(cubic_monomials&#91;index&#93;) for index in pivot_columns
            &#93;,
            "nonpivot_column_count": len(nonpivot_columns),
            "schur_complement_equation_count": L.rows,
        },
        "quadratic_kuranishi": {
            "parameter_order": parameter_names,
            "candidate_monomial_count": len(monomial_pairs),
            "nonzero_obstruction_monomials": nonzero_monomials,
            "obstruction_coefficient_rank": obstruction_rank,
            "independent_equation_count": len(equations),
            "equations": compact_equations,
        },
        "projective_eta_ratio_analysis": {
            "u_axis": {
                "matrix_rank": axis_u_ranks&#91;0&#93;,
                "augmented_rank": axis_u_ranks&#91;1&#93;,
                "second_order_solvable": axis_u_ranks&#91;0&#93; == axis_u_ranks&#91;1&#93;,
            },
            "v_axis": {
                "matrix_rank": axis_v_ranks&#91;0&#93;,
                "augmented_rank": axis_v_ranks&#91;1&#93;,
                "second_order_solvable": axis_v_ranks&#91;0&#93; == axis_v_ranks&#91;1&#93;,
            },
            "generic_v_nonzero": {
                "coefficient_field": "Q(t), t=u/v",
                "matrix_rank": generic_rank,
                "augmented_rank": generic_augmented_rank,
                "second_order_solvable_generically": (
                    generic_rank == generic_augmented_rank
                ),
            },
            "rational_samples": sample_ratios,
        },
        "interpretation_boundary": (
            "Vanishing of the quadratic Kuranishi equations is necessary and "
            "sufficient for a rank-six arc through second order in the chosen "
            "Schur-complement chart. It does not establish all-order "
            "integration, polynomial realization, or the true operation-group "
            "quotient."
        ),
    }


def main(argv: Sequence&#91;str&#93; | None = None) -&gt; int:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument("--source", type=Path, default=DEFAULT_SOURCE)
    parser.add_argument("--output", type=Path)
    args = parser.parse_args(argv)
    try:
        result = analyze_second_order(args.source)
    except (OSError, ValueError, TypeError, AssertionError) as exc:
        print(f"program5_rank_six_second_order: {exc}", file=sys.stderr)
        return 2
    rendered = json.dumps(result, indent=2, sort_keys=True) + "\n"
    if args.output:
        args.output.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        args.output.write_text(rendered, encoding="utf-8")
    else:
        sys.stdout.write(rendered)
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())
</code></pre>

## `research-tools/filtered_operation_complex/adapters/program5_rank_six_third_order_lift.py`

<pre><code class="language-python">
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Test the Program 5 cubic rank-six obstruction modulo tangent freedom.

The deterministic formal lift in ``program5_rank_six_formal_arc.py`` sets the
22-dimensional homogeneous freedom in each quadratic correction equal to
zero.  Its order-three forcing has a nonzero cokernel projection.  That
deterministic residual alone does not decide whether the obstruction is
intrinsic, because every order-two correction is defined only modulo the
rank-six tangent kernel.

This script introduces all 66 free coefficients

    K_rank tensor Sym^2&lt;u,v&gt;

in the three quadratic corrections, computes their exact effect on the four
cubic forcing coefficients, and tests the resulting linear system over Q.
For the current source, the system has rank 15 and augmented rank 16.  The
script returns an exact left-null certificate showing that the chosen
first-order plane cannot be lifted through cubic parameter order.

The result decides compatibility through cubic parameter order for the chosen
two-dimensional first-order plane. It does not decide fourth order, all-order
integration, convergence, or the true operation-group quotient.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import hashlib
import json
import sys
from itertools import combinations_with_replacement
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Sequence

import sympy as sp
from sympy.polys.matrices import DomainMatrix

from .program5_compression_export import (
    DEFAULT_SOURCE,
    _load_source,
    _monomial_label,
    _q,
    build_export,
)
from .program5_rank_six_second_order import (
    _flatten,
    _independent_rows_and_columns,
    _matrix_rank,
    _project_to_cokernel,
    _sympy_matrix,
)
from .program5_tangent_bridge import _extend_basis


def _sparse_vector(
    vector: sp.Matrix,
    labels: Sequence&#91;str&#93;,
) -&gt; list&#91;dict&#91;str, Any&#93;&#93;:
    return &#91;
        {"coordinate": labels&#91;index&#93;, "coefficient": _q(vector&#91;index, 0&#93;)}
        for index in range(vector.rows)
        if vector&#91;index, 0&#93; != 0
    &#93;


def _digest(vector: sp.Matrix) -&gt; str:
    return hashlib.sha256(
        json.dumps(
            &#91;str(sp.Rational(value)) for value in vector&#93;,
            separators=(",", ":"),
        ).encode("utf-8")
    ).hexdigest()


def _deterministic_linear_solution(
    matrix: sp.Matrix,
    rhs: sp.Matrix,
) -&gt; tuple&#91;bool, sp.Matrix | None, int, int, int&#93;:
    rank = DomainMatrix.from_Matrix(matrix, fmt="sparse").rank()
    augmented_rank = DomainMatrix.from_Matrix(
        matrix.row_join(rhs),
        fmt="sparse",
    ).rank()
    if rank != augmented_rank:
        return False, None, rank, augmented_rank, matrix.cols - rank
    solution_tuple = next(iter(sp.linsolve((matrix, rhs))))
    free_symbols = sorted(
        set().union(*(entry.free_symbols for entry in solution_tuple)),
        key=str,
    )
    substitution = {symbol: 0 for symbol in free_symbols}
    solution = sp.Matrix(
        &#91;sp.factor(entry.subs(substitution)) for entry in solution_tuple&#93;
    )
    if matrix * solution != rhs:
        raise AssertionError("deterministic cubic-lift solution failed")
    return True, solution, rank, augmented_rank, len(free_symbols)


def analyze_third_order(source_path: Path = DEFAULT_SOURCE) -&gt; dict&#91;str, Any&#93;:
    contract, exported = build_export(source_path)
    row_layer, rank_layer = contract&#91;"layers"&#93;
    operation_labels = list(row_layer&#91;"deformation_basis"&#93;)
    row_kernel = _sympy_matrix(row_layer&#91;"actions"&#93;&#91;0&#93;&#91;"action_matrix"&#93;)
    rank_kernel = _sympy_matrix(rank_layer&#91;"actions"&#93;&#91;0&#93;&#91;"action_matrix"&#93;)
    row_columns = &#91;row_kernel&#91;:, index&#93; for index in range(row_kernel.cols)&#93;
    rank_columns = &#91;rank_kernel&#91;:, index&#93; for index in range(rank_kernel.cols)&#93;
    adapted, complement = _extend_basis(row_columns, rank_columns)
    if len(adapted) != 22 or len(complement) != 2:
        raise AssertionError("the 20+2 tangent splitting changed")
    tangent_basis = adapted
    eta0, eta1 = complement
    theta_u = eta0 + row_columns&#91;4&#93;
    theta_v = eta1 + 4 * row_columns&#91;0&#93; - 24 * row_columns&#91;1&#93; - 4 * row_columns&#91;4&#93;

    source, _ = _load_source(source_path)
    V = tuple(source.V)
    Q = sp.Matrix(source.Q)
    C = sp.Matrix(source.C)
    weights = source.weights
    n = len(V)
    cubic_monomials = &#91;
        sp.prod(V&#91;index&#93; for index in indices)
        for indices in combinations_with_replacement(range(n), 3)
    &#93;
    quadratic_monomials = &#91;
        sp.prod(V&#91;index&#93; for index in indices)
        for indices in combinations_with_replacement(range(n), 2)
    &#93;

    def weight(monomial: sp.Expr) -&gt; int:
        exponents = sp.Poly(monomial, *V).monoms()&#91;0&#93;
        return sum(exponents&#91;index&#93; * weights&#91;V&#91;index&#93;&#93; for index in range(n))

    operation_basis: list&#91;tuple&#91;int, sp.Expr&#93;&#93; = &#91;&#93;
    for row, variable in enumerate(V):
        for monomial in quadratic_monomials:
            if weight(monomial) == weights&#91;variable&#93;:
                operation_basis.append((row, monomial))
    labels = &#91;
        f"e_{V&#91;row&#93;}*{_monomial_label(monomial)}"
        for row, monomial in operation_basis
    &#93;
    if len(operation_basis) != 115 or labels != operation_labels:
        raise AssertionError("operation basis changed")

    JQ = Q.jacobian(V)
    variation_matrices: list&#91;sp.Matrix&#93; = &#91;&#93;
    for operation_row, monomial in operation_basis:
        field = sp.zeros(n, 1)
        field&#91;operation_row, 0&#93; = monomial
        bracket = sp.Matrix(
            &#91;
                sp.expand(value)
                for value in JQ * field - field.jacobian(V) * Q
            &#93;
        )
        coefficient_matrix = sp.zeros(n, len(cubic_monomials))
        for row, expression in enumerate(bracket):
            polynomial = sp.Poly(expression, *V)
            for column, cubic_monomial in enumerate(cubic_monomials):
                coefficient_matrix&#91;row, column&#93; = polynomial.coeff_monomial(
                    cubic_monomial
                )
        variation_matrices.append(coefficient_matrix)

    def variation(vector: sp.Matrix) -&gt; sp.Matrix:
        result = sp.zeros(n, len(cubic_monomials))
        for coefficient, matrix in zip(vector, variation_matrices):
            if coefficient:
                result += coefficient * matrix
        return result

    p0 = sp.zeros(115, 1)
    p0_index = next(
        index
        for index, (row, monomial) in enumerate(operation_basis)
        if row == 3 and sp.expand(monomial - source.d**2) == 0
    )
    p0&#91;p0_index, 0&#93; = -1
    M0 = sp.zeros(n, len(cubic_monomials))
    for row, expression in enumerate(C):
        polynomial = sp.Poly(expression, *V)
        for column, monomial in enumerate(cubic_monomials):
            M0&#91;row, column&#93; = polynomial.coeff_monomial(monomial)
    M0 += variation(p0)

    base_rows = &#91;0, 1, 2, 4, 5, 8&#93;
    zero_rows = &#91;3, 6, 7, 9, 10&#93;
    base_matrix = M0&#91;base_rows, :&#93;
    _, pivot_tuple = base_matrix.rref()
    pivot_columns = list(pivot_tuple)
    nonpivot_columns = &#91;
        column for column in range(len(cubic_monomials))
        if column not in pivot_columns
    &#93;
    A0 = M0&#91;base_rows, pivot_columns&#93;
    B0 = M0&#91;base_rows, nonpivot_columns&#93;
    G0 = A0.inv()

    def blocks(vector: sp.Matrix) -&gt; dict&#91;str, sp.Matrix&#93;:
        matrix = variation(vector)
        return {
            "A": matrix&#91;base_rows, pivot_columns&#93;,
            "B": matrix&#91;base_rows, nonpivot_columns&#93;,
            "C": matrix&#91;zero_rows, pivot_columns&#93;,
            "D": matrix&#91;zero_rows, nonpivot_columns&#93;,
        }

    ambient_blocks = &#91;blocks(sp.eye(115)&#91;:, index&#93;) for index in range(115)&#93;
    L_columns = &#91;
        _flatten(block&#91;"D"&#93; - block&#91;"C"&#93; * G0 * B0)
        for block in ambient_blocks
    &#93;
    L = sp.Matrix.hstack(*L_columns)
    if _matrix_rank(L) != 93:
        raise AssertionError("tangent map rank changed")
    for vector in tangent_basis:
        if L * vector != sp.zeros(L.rows, 1):
            raise AssertionError("exported tangent basis left ker(L)")
    pivot_rows, pivot_operation_columns = _independent_rows_and_columns(L)
    minor = L&#91;pivot_rows, pivot_operation_columns&#93;
    minor_inverse = minor.inv()

    def solve_image(forcing: sp.Matrix) -&gt; tuple&#91;sp.Matrix, sp.Matrix&#93;:
        residual = _project_to_cokernel(
            forcing,
            L,
            pivot_rows=pivot_rows,
            pivot_columns=pivot_operation_columns,
            inverse_minor=minor_inverse,
        )
        if any(residual):
            raise AssertionError("forcing is not in the tangent image")
        coefficients = minor_inverse * forcing&#91;list(pivot_rows), :&#93;
        correction = sp.zeros(115, 1)
        for index, column in enumerate(pivot_operation_columns):
            correction&#91;column, 0&#93; = coefficients&#91;index, 0&#93;
        if L * correction != forcing:
            raise AssertionError("deterministic image solution failed")
        return correction, residual

    theta_blocks = &#91;blocks(theta_v), blocks(theta_u)&#93;
    G1 = &#91;
        -G0 * theta_blocks&#91;index&#93;&#91;"A"&#93; * G0
        for index in range(2)
    &#93;

    # H2 coefficients in order v^2, u*v, u^2.
    H2 = &#91;&#93;
    for total_u in range(3):
        value = sp.zeros(len(zero_rows), len(nonpivot_columns))
        for left_u in range(2):
            right_u = total_u - left_u
            if 0 &lt;= right_u &lt; 2:
                value += (
                    theta_blocks&#91;left_u&#93;&#91;"C"&#93;
                    * G0
                    * theta_blocks&#91;right_u&#93;&#91;"B"&#93;
                )
        for c_u in range(2):
            g_u = total_u - c_u
            if 0 &lt;= g_u &lt; 2:
                value += theta_blocks&#91;c_u&#93;&#91;"C"&#93; * G1&#91;g_u&#93; * B0
        H2.append(value)

    P2_particular = &#91;&#93;
    for value in H2:
        correction, _ = solve_image(_flatten(value))
        P2_particular.append(correction)

    def inverse_order_two_blocks(
        P2_blocks: Sequence&#91;dict&#91;str, sp.Matrix&#93;&#93;,
    ) -&gt; list&#91;sp.Matrix&#93;:
        A2 = &#91;block&#91;"A"&#93; for block in P2_blocks&#93;
        result = &#91;&#93;
        for total_u in range(3):
            convolution = sp.zeros(6, 6)
            for left_u in range(2):
                right_u = total_u - left_u
                if 0 &lt;= right_u &lt; 2:
                    convolution += theta_blocks&#91;left_u&#93;&#91;"A"&#93; * G1&#91;right_u&#93;
            convolution += A2&#91;total_u&#93; * G0
            result.append(-G0 * convolution)
        return result

    def cubic_forcing_blocks(
        P2_blocks: Sequence&#91;dict&#91;str, sp.Matrix&#93;&#93;,
    ) -&gt; list&#91;sp.Matrix&#93;:
        G2 = inverse_order_two_blocks(P2_blocks)
        values = &#91;&#93;
        for total_u in range(4):
            value = sp.zeros(len(zero_rows), len(nonpivot_columns))
            # Sum C_i G_j B_k for i+j+k=3, i&gt;=1, using orders 1 and 2.
            for i_order in (1, 2):
                C_blocks = theta_blocks if i_order == 1 else P2_blocks
                C_list = &#91;block&#91;"C"&#93; for block in C_blocks&#93;
                for j_order in (0, 1, 2):
                    k_order = 3 - i_order - j_order
                    if k_order not in (0, 1, 2):
                        continue
                    G_list = &#91;G0&#93; if j_order == 0 else (G1 if j_order == 1 else G2)
                    if k_order == 0:
                        B_list = &#91;B0&#93;
                    elif k_order == 1:
                        B_list = &#91;block&#91;"B"&#93; for block in theta_blocks&#93;
                    else:
                        B_list = &#91;block&#91;"B"&#93; for block in P2_blocks&#93;
                    for c_u, C_value in enumerate(C_list):
                        for g_u, G_value in enumerate(G_list):
                            b_u = total_u - c_u - g_u
                            if 0 &lt;= b_u &lt; len(B_list):
                                value += C_value * G_value * B_list&#91;b_u&#93;
            values.append(value)
        return values

    def cubic_forcing(P2: Sequence&#91;sp.Matrix&#93;) -&gt; list&#91;sp.Matrix&#93;:
        return cubic_forcing_blocks(&#91;blocks(vector) for vector in P2&#93;)

    base_H3 = cubic_forcing(P2_particular)
    base_residuals = &#91;
        _project_to_cokernel(
            _flatten(value),
            L,
            pivot_rows=pivot_rows,
            pivot_columns=pivot_operation_columns,
            inverse_minor=minor_inverse,
        )
        for value in base_H3
    &#93;
    if sum(any(vector) for vector in base_residuals) != 4:
        raise AssertionError("the deterministic cubic obstruction changed")

    # Exact linear effect of a tangent-kernel addition to P2.
    tangent_blocks = &#91;blocks(vector) for vector in tangent_basis&#93;

    def bilinear_h(
        left: dict&#91;str, sp.Matrix&#93;,
        right: dict&#91;str, sp.Matrix&#93;,
    ) -&gt; sp.Matrix:
        return (
            left&#91;"C"&#93; * G0 * right&#91;"B"&#93;
            - left&#91;"C"&#93; * G0 * right&#91;"A"&#93; * G0 * B0
            + right&#91;"C"&#93; * G0 * left&#91;"B"&#93;
            - right&#91;"C"&#93; * G0 * left&#91;"A"&#93; * G0 * B0
        )

    effect_v = &#91;&#93;
    effect_u = &#91;&#93;
    for tangent_block in tangent_blocks:
        effect_v.append(
            _project_to_cokernel(
                _flatten(bilinear_h(tangent_block, theta_blocks&#91;0&#93;)),
                L,
                pivot_rows=pivot_rows,
                pivot_columns=pivot_operation_columns,
                inverse_minor=minor_inverse,
            )
        )
        effect_u.append(
            _project_to_cokernel(
                _flatten(bilinear_h(tangent_block, theta_blocks&#91;1&#93;)),
                L,
                pivot_rows=pivot_rows,
                pivot_columns=pivot_operation_columns,
                inverse_minor=minor_inverse,
            )
        )

    # Independently replay every column of the linearized effect by adding a
    # single tangent basis vector to one quadratic coefficient and recomputing
    # the complete cubic forcing.  This guards the closed bilinear formula and
    # its u/v monomial bookkeeping.
    particular_blocks = &#91;blocks(vector) for vector in P2_particular&#93;
    direct_effect_columns_verified = 0
    for input_u in range(3):
        for tangent_index, tangent_block in enumerate(tangent_blocks):
            perturbed_blocks = &#91;
                {name: matrix.copy() for name, matrix in block.items()}
                for block in particular_blocks
            &#93;
            for name, matrix in tangent_block.items():
                perturbed_blocks&#91;input_u&#93;&#91;name&#93; += matrix
            direct_values = cubic_forcing_blocks(perturbed_blocks)
            direct_residuals = &#91;
                _project_to_cokernel(
                    _flatten(value),
                    L,
                    pivot_rows=pivot_rows,
                    pivot_columns=pivot_operation_columns,
                    inverse_minor=minor_inverse,
                )
                for value in direct_values
            &#93;
            for output_u in range(4):
                expected = sp.zeros(L.rows, 1)
                if output_u == input_u:
                    expected += effect_v&#91;tangent_index&#93;
                if output_u == input_u + 1:
                    expected += effect_u&#91;tangent_index&#93;
                observed = direct_residuals&#91;output_u&#93; - base_residuals&#91;output_u&#93;
                if observed != expected:
                    raise AssertionError(
                        "direct cubic-effect replay disagrees with the "
                        f"bilinear formula in column {22 * input_u + tangent_index}"
                    )
            direct_effect_columns_verified += 1

    # Compress the 4*1400 equations to the rows that can be nonzero.
    row_keys: set&#91;tuple&#91;int, int&#93;&#93; = set()
    for output_u, residual in enumerate(base_residuals):
        row_keys.update(
            (output_u, row)
            for row, value in enumerate(residual)
            if value
        )
    for input_u in range(3):
        for effect in effect_v:
            row_keys.update(
                (input_u, row)
                for row, value in enumerate(effect)
                if value
            )
        for effect in effect_u:
            row_keys.update(
                (input_u + 1, row)
                for row, value in enumerate(effect)
                if value
            )
    ordered_rows = sorted(row_keys)
    row_index = {key: index for index, key in enumerate(ordered_rows)}
    effect_matrix = sp.zeros(len(ordered_rows), 66)
    rhs = sp.zeros(len(ordered_rows), 1)
    for output_u, residual in enumerate(base_residuals):
        for row, value in enumerate(residual):
            if value:
                rhs&#91;row_index&#91;(output_u, row)&#93;, 0&#93; = -value
    for input_u in range(3):
        for tangent_index in range(22):
            column = 22 * input_u + tangent_index
            for row, value in enumerate(effect_v&#91;tangent_index&#93;):
                if value:
                    effect_matrix&#91;row_index&#91;(input_u, row)&#93;, column&#93; += value
            for row, value in enumerate(effect_u&#91;tangent_index&#93;):
                if value:
                    effect_matrix&#91;row_index&#91;(input_u + 1, row)&#93;, column&#93; += value

    solvable, solution, effect_rank, augmented_rank, free_dimension = (
        _deterministic_linear_solution(effect_matrix, rhs)
    )
    obstruction_certificate: dict&#91;str, Any&#93; | None = None
    if not solvable:
        left_null_basis = effect_matrix.T.nullspace()
        witness = next(
            (
                vector
                for vector in left_null_basis
                if (vector.T * rhs)&#91;0, 0&#93; != 0
            ),
            None,
        )
        if witness is None:
            raise AssertionError("rank mismatch has no left-null witness")
        first_nonzero = next(value for value in witness if value != 0)
        witness = sp.Matrix(&#91;sp.factor(value / first_nonzero) for value in witness&#93;)
        pairing = sp.factor((witness.T * rhs)&#91;0, 0&#93;)
        if witness.T * effect_matrix != sp.zeros(1, effect_matrix.cols):
            raise AssertionError("cubic obstruction witness left the left nullspace")
        if pairing == 0:
            raise AssertionError("cubic obstruction witness does not separate the rhs")
        parameter_monomials = ("v^3", "u*v^2", "u^2*v", "u^3")
        certificate_coordinates = &#91;&#93;
        for certificate_row, coefficient in enumerate(witness):
            if coefficient == 0:
                continue
            output_u, residual_row = ordered_rows&#91;certificate_row&#93;
            schur_row, schur_column = divmod(
                residual_row,
                len(nonpivot_columns),
            )
            certificate_coordinates.append(
                {
                    "parameter_monomial": parameter_monomials&#91;output_u&#93;,
                    "schur_row_variable": str(V&#91;zero_rows&#91;schur_row&#93;&#93;),
                    "schur_column_monomial": _monomial_label(
                        cubic_monomials&#91;nonpivot_columns&#91;schur_column&#93;&#93;
                    ),
                    "coefficient": _q(coefficient),
                }
            )
        obstruction_certificate = {
            "kind": "exact left-null witness",
            "equation_order": list(parameter_monomials),
            "nonzero_count": len(certificate_coordinates),
            "pairing_with_rhs": _q(pairing),
            "witness_sha256": _digest(witness),
            "coordinates": certificate_coordinates,
        }
    result: dict&#91;str, Any&#93; = {
        "schema_version": 2,
        "name": "Program 5 cubic lift modulo quadratic tangent freedom",
        "source_file": str(source_path),
        "source_sha256": exported&#91;"summary"&#93;&#91;"source_sha256"&#93;,
        "ambient_operation_dimension": 115,
        "rank_six_tangent_dimension": 22,
        "quadratic_coefficient_count": 3,
        "quadratic_tangent_freedom_dimension": 66,
        "direct_effect_columns_verified": direct_effect_columns_verified,
        "compressed_cubic_equation_count": len(ordered_rows),
        "cubic_effect_rank": effect_rank,
        "cubic_augmented_rank": augmented_rank,
        "cubic_lift_solvable": solvable,
        "cubic_lift_solution_free_dimension": free_dimension if solvable else None,
        "deterministic_base_obstructed_monomials": &#91;
            "v^3",
            "u*v^2",
            "u^2*v",
            "u^3",
        &#93;,
    }
    if not solvable or solution is None:
        result&#91;"cubic_obstruction_certificate"&#93; = obstruction_certificate
        result&#91;"interpretation"&#93; = (
            "The cubic obstruction survives every tangent-kernel choice in "
            "the quadratic correction, so it is intrinsic for the chosen "
            "first-order plane."
        )
        return result

    adjusted_P2 = &#91;vector.copy() for vector in P2_particular&#93;
    tangent_adjustments = &#91;&#93;
    for input_u in range(3):
        adjustment = sp.zeros(115, 1)
        coefficients = &#91;&#93;
        for tangent_index, tangent_vector in enumerate(tangent_basis):
            coefficient = solution&#91;22 * input_u + tangent_index, 0&#93;
            if coefficient:
                adjustment += coefficient * tangent_vector
                coefficients.append(
                    {
                        "tangent_basis_index": tangent_index,
                        "coefficient": _q(coefficient),
                    }
                )
        adjusted_P2&#91;input_u&#93; += adjustment
        tangent_adjustments.append(
            {
                "monomial": ("v^2", "u*v", "u^2")&#91;input_u&#93;,
                "coefficient_count": len(coefficients),
                "coefficients": coefficients,
                "adjustment_nonzero_count": len(_sparse_vector(adjustment, labels)),
                "adjustment_sha256": _digest(adjustment),
            }
        )

    adjusted_H3 = cubic_forcing(adjusted_P2)
    adjusted_residuals = &#91;
        _project_to_cokernel(
            _flatten(value),
            L,
            pivot_rows=pivot_rows,
            pivot_columns=pivot_operation_columns,
            inverse_minor=minor_inverse,
        )
        for value in adjusted_H3
    &#93;
    if any(any(vector) for vector in adjusted_residuals):
        raise AssertionError("the solved tangent adjustment did not kill the cubic obstruction")
    P3 = &#91;&#93;
    for value in adjusted_H3:
        correction, _ = solve_image(_flatten(value))
        P3.append(correction)

    result.update(
        {
            "quadratic_tangent_adjustments": tangent_adjustments,
            "adjusted_quadratic_corrections": &#91;
                {
                    "monomial": ("v^2", "u*v", "u^2")&#91;index&#93;,
                    "nonzero_count": len(_sparse_vector(vector, labels)),
                    "sha256": _digest(vector),
                    "vector": _sparse_vector(vector, labels),
                }
                for index, vector in enumerate(adjusted_P2)
            &#93;,
            "cubic_obstruction_after_adjustment": 0,
            "cubic_corrections": &#91;
                {
                    "monomial": (
                        "v^3",
                        "u*v^2",
                        "u^2*v",
                        "u^3",
                    )&#91;index&#93;,
                    "nonzero_count": len(_sparse_vector(vector, labels)),
                    "sha256": _digest(vector),
                    "vector": _sparse_vector(vector, labels),
                }
                for index, vector in enumerate(P3)
            &#93;,
            "interpretation": (
                "The deterministic cubic obstruction is removable by the "
                "22-dimensional tangent freedom in the quadratic correction. "
                "The chosen two-dimensional branch therefore survives through "
                "cubic parameter order."
            ),
        }
    )
    return result


def main(argv: Sequence&#91;str&#93; | None = None) -&gt; int:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument("--source", type=Path, default=DEFAULT_SOURCE)
    parser.add_argument("--output", type=Path)
    args = parser.parse_args(argv)
    try:
        result = analyze_third_order(args.source)
    except (OSError, ValueError, TypeError, AssertionError) as exc:
        print(f"program5_rank_six_third_order_lift: {exc}", file=sys.stderr)
        return 2
    rendered = json.dumps(result, indent=2, sort_keys=True) + "\n"
    if args.output:
        args.output.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        args.output.write_text(rendered, encoding="utf-8")
    else:
        sys.stdout.write(rendered)
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())
