## `research-tools/filtered_operation_complex/symbolic_tests/test_program5_algebraic_fourth_order_kuranishi.py`

<pre><code class="language-python">
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import sys
import unittest
from pathlib import Path

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
RESEARCH_TOOLS = HERE.parent.parent
sys.path.insert(0, str(RESEARCH_TOOLS))

from filtered_operation_complex.adapters.program5_rank_six_algebraic_fourth_order_kuranishi import (  # noqa: E402
    analyze_algebraic_fourth_order_kuranishi,
)


class Program5AlgebraicFourthOrderKuranishiTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_complete_algebraic_cubic_lift_fibre_is_retained(self) -&gt; None:
        result = analyze_algebraic_fourth_order_kuranishi()

        self.assertEqual(result&#91;"schema_version"&#93;, 1)
        self.assertEqual(
            result&#91;"source_sha256"&#93;,
            "a2ec1fb45cc42b527958f3c881b8a9bb8c0ce093aa553c846806ab868513a2d8",
        )
        self.assertEqual(result&#91;"coefficient_field"&#93;, "Q(sqrt(-3))")
        self.assertTrue(
            result&#91;"conjugate_result_follows_by_Q_galois_symmetry"&#93;
        )
        self.assertEqual(result&#91;"rank_six_tangent_dimension"&#93;, 22)
        self.assertEqual(result&#91;"cubic_effect_rank"&#93;, 5)
        self.assertEqual(result&#91;"cubic_augmented_rank"&#93;, 5)
        self.assertEqual(result&#91;"cubic_lift_affine_dimension"&#93;, 17)

        order_four = result&#91;"order_four_map"&#93;
        self.assertEqual(order_four&#91;"source_dimension"&#93;, 17)
        self.assertEqual(order_four&#91;"linear_coefficient_count"&#93;, 17)
        self.assertEqual(
            order_four&#91;"diagonal_quadratic_coefficient_count"&#93;,
            17,
        )
        self.assertEqual(
            order_four&#91;"off_diagonal_quadratic_coefficient_count"&#93;,
            136,
        )
        self.assertGreater(order_four&#91;"active_target_coordinate_count"&#93;, 0)
        self.assertEqual(
            result&#91;"intrinsic_order_four_obstruction"&#93;,
            order_four&#91;
                "constant_term_outside_variable_coefficient_span"
            &#93;,
        )
        if result&#91;"intrinsic_order_four_obstruction"&#93;:
            self.assertGreater(
                order_four&#91;"constant_augmented_rank"&#93;,
                order_four&#91;"variable_coefficient_rank"&#93;,
            )
            certificate = result&#91;"obstruction_certificate"&#93;
            self.assertIsNotNone(certificate)
            self.assertNotEqual(
                certificate&#91;"pairing_with_constant_term"&#93;,
                "0",
            )
            self.assertGreater(certificate&#91;"nonzero_count"&#93;, 0)
        else:
            self.assertEqual(
                order_four&#91;"constant_augmented_rank"&#93;,
                order_four&#91;"variable_coefficient_rank"&#93;,
            )
            self.assertIsNone(result&#91;"obstruction_certificate"&#93;)

        print(
            json.dumps(
                {
                    "audited_ratio": result&#91;"audited_ratio"&#93;,
                    "conjugate_ratio": result&#91;"conjugate_ratio"&#93;,
                    "cubic_effect_rank": result&#91;"cubic_effect_rank"&#93;,
                    "cubic_lift_affine_dimension": result&#91;
                        "cubic_lift_affine_dimension"
                    &#93;,
                    "constant_nonzero_count": order_four&#91;
                        "constant_nonzero_count"
                    &#93;,
                    "active_target_coordinate_count": order_four&#91;
                        "active_target_coordinate_count"
                    &#93;,
                    "variable_coefficient_rank": order_four&#91;
                        "variable_coefficient_rank"
                    &#93;,
                    "constant_augmented_rank": order_four&#91;
                        "constant_augmented_rank"
                    &#93;,
                    "intrinsic_order_four_obstruction": result&#91;
                        "intrinsic_order_four_obstruction"
                    &#93;,
                    "certificate_pairing": (
                        result&#91;"obstruction_certificate"&#93; or {}
                    ).get("pairing_with_constant_term"),
                    "certificate_nonzero_count": (
                        result&#91;"obstruction_certificate"&#93; or {}
                    ).get("nonzero_count"),
                },
                indent=2,
                sort_keys=True,
            )
        )


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()
</code></pre>

## `research-tools/filtered_operation_complex/symbolic_tests/test_program5_exceptional_finite_lines.py`

<pre><code class="language-python">
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import sys
import unittest
from pathlib import Path

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
RESEARCH_TOOLS = HERE.parent.parent
sys.path.insert(0, str(RESEARCH_TOOLS))

from filtered_operation_complex.adapters.program5_rank_six_exceptional_finite_lines import (  # noqa: E402
    analyze_exceptional_finite_lines,
)


class Program5ExceptionalFiniteLineTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_all_pairing_ideal_roots_are_specialized_exactly(self) -&gt; None:
        result = analyze_exceptional_finite_lines()

        self.assertEqual(result&#91;"schema_version"&#93;, 1)
        self.assertEqual(
            result&#91;"source_sha256"&#93;,
            "a2ec1fb45cc42b527958f3c881b8a9bb8c0ce093aa553c846806ab868513a2d8",
        )
        self.assertEqual(result&#91;"exceptional_ratio_count"&#93;, 3)
        ratios = result&#91;"exceptional_ratios"&#93;
        self.assertEqual(len(ratios), 3)
        self.assertTrue(result&#91;"conjugate_rank_profiles_equal"&#93;)
        for item in ratios:
            self.assertEqual(
                item&#91;"cubic_lift_solvable"&#93;,
                item&#91;"cubic_effect_rank"&#93;
                == item&#91;"cubic_augmented_rank"&#93;,
            )
        self.assertEqual(
            result&#91;"all_exceptional_finite_ratios_obstructed"&#93;,
            all(not item&#91;"cubic_lift_solvable"&#93; for item in ratios),
        )
        self.assertEqual(
            result&#91;"finite_selected_plane_closed_at_cubic_order"&#93;,
            result&#91;"all_exceptional_finite_ratios_obstructed"&#93;,
        )

        print(
            json.dumps(
                {
                    "generic_pairing_polynomial": result&#91;
                        "generic_pairing_polynomial"
                    &#93;,
                    "exceptional_ratios": &#91;
                        {
                            "label": item&#91;"label"&#93;,
                            "field": item&#91;"field"&#93;,
                            "rank": item&#91;"cubic_effect_rank"&#93;,
                            "augmented_rank": item&#91;
                                "cubic_augmented_rank"
                            &#93;,
                            "solvable": item&#91;"cubic_lift_solvable"&#93;,
                        }
                        for item in ratios
                    &#93;,
                    "finite_selected_plane_closed": result&#91;
                        "finite_selected_plane_closed_at_cubic_order"
                    &#93;,
                },
                indent=2,
                sort_keys=True,
            )
        )


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()
</code></pre>

## `research-tools/filtered_operation_complex/symbolic_tests/test_program5_fourth_order_kuranishi.py`

<pre><code class="language-python">
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import sys
import unittest
from pathlib import Path

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
RESEARCH_TOOLS = HERE.parent.parent
sys.path.insert(0, str(RESEARCH_TOOLS))

from filtered_operation_complex.adapters.program5_rank_six_fourth_order_kuranishi import (  # noqa: E402
    analyze_fourth_order_kuranishi,
)


class Program5FourthOrderKuranishiTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_complete_cubic_lift_fibre_is_retained_at_order_four(self) -&gt; None:
        result = analyze_fourth_order_kuranishi()

        self.assertEqual(result&#91;"schema_version"&#93;, 1)
        self.assertEqual(
            result&#91;"source_sha256"&#93;,
            "a2ec1fb45cc42b527958f3c881b8a9bb8c0ce093aa553c846806ab868513a2d8",
        )
        self.assertEqual(result&#91;"rank_six_tangent_dimension"&#93;, 22)
        self.assertEqual(result&#91;"cubic_effect_rank"&#93;, 2)
        self.assertEqual(result&#91;"cubic_augmented_rank"&#93;, 2)
        self.assertEqual(result&#91;"cubic_lift_affine_dimension"&#93;, 20)
        self.assertGreater(
            result&#91;"deterministic_quartic_residual"&#93;&#91;"nonzero_count"&#93;,
            0,
        )

        kuranishi = result&#91;"quartic_kuranishi"&#93;
        self.assertEqual(kuranishi&#91;"source_dimension"&#93;, 20)
        self.assertGreater(kuranishi&#91;"target_coefficient_rank"&#93;, 0)
        self.assertEqual(
            kuranishi&#91;"independent_equation_count"&#93;,
            len(kuranishi&#91;"equations"&#93;),
        )
        for equation in kuranishi&#91;"equations"&#93;:
            self.assertLessEqual(equation&#91;"total_degree"&#93;, 2)
            self.assertGreater(len(equation&#91;"terms"&#93;), 0)

        self.assertEqual(
            result&#91;"rational_zero_found"&#93;,
            result&#91;"rational_zero"&#93; is not None,
        )
        if result&#91;"rational_zero_found"&#93;:
            self.assertTrue(result&#91;"rational_zero_verified"&#93;)
            self.assertGreaterEqual(
                result&#91;"rational_zero"&#93;&#91;"coefficient_count"&#93;,
                0,
            )
        else:
            self.assertFalse(result&#91;"rational_zero_verified"&#93;)

        print(
            json.dumps(
                {
                    "cubic_effect_rank": result&#91;"cubic_effect_rank"&#93;,
                    "cubic_lift_affine_dimension": result&#91;
                        "cubic_lift_affine_dimension"
                    &#93;,
                    "deterministic_quartic_residual_nonzero_count": result&#91;
                        "deterministic_quartic_residual"
                    &#93;&#91;"nonzero_count"&#93;,
                    "quartic_target_coefficient_rank": kuranishi&#91;
                        "target_coefficient_rank"
                    &#93;,
                    "quartic_equations": &#91;
                        {
                            "row_variable": equation&#91;
                                "schur_row_variable"
                            &#93;,
                            "column_monomial": equation&#91;
                                "schur_column_monomial"
                            &#93;,
                            "expression": equation&#91;"expression"&#93;,
                            "term_count": len(equation&#91;"terms"&#93;),
                        }
                        for equation in kuranishi&#91;"equations"&#93;
                    &#93;,
                    "rational_zero_found": result&#91;"rational_zero_found"&#93;,
                    "rational_zero": result&#91;"rational_zero"&#93;,
                },
                indent=2,
                sort_keys=True,
            )
        )


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()
</code></pre>

<pre><code class="language-python">
from __future__ import annotations

    analyze_generic_line_obstruction,

class Program5GenericLineObstructionTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_finite_ratio_obstruction_is_computed_symbolically(self) -&gt; None:
        result = analyze_generic_line_obstruction()

        self.assertEqual(result&#91;"schema_version"&#93;, 2)
        self.assertEqual(
            result&#91;"source_sha256"&#93;,
            "a2ec1fb45cc42b527958f3c881b8a9bb8c0ce093aa553c846806ab868513a2d8",
        )
        self.assertTrue(result&#91;"second_order_residual_identically_zero"&#93;)
        self.assertEqual(result&#91;"rank_six_tangent_dimension"&#93;, 22)
        self.assertEqual(result&#91;"left_kernel_dimension_over_Q(r)"&#93;, 2)
        self.assertEqual(
            result&#91;"generic_augmented_rank_over_Q(r)"&#93;,
            result&#91;"generic_effect_rank_over_Q(r)"&#93; + 1,
        )

        basis = result&#91;"primitive_left_kernel_basis"&#93;
        self.assertEqual(len(basis), 2)
        for witness in basis:
            self.assertEqual(witness&#91;"coordinate_gcd"&#93;, "1")
            self.assertGreater(witness&#91;"nonzero_count"&#93;, 0)

        pairing_ideal = result&#91;"pairing_ideal"&#93;
        self.assertNotEqual(pairing_ideal&#91;"generator"&#93;, "0")
        certificate = result&#91;"combined_bezout_certificate"&#93;
        self.assertGreater(certificate&#91;"nonzero_count"&#93;, 0)
        self.assertNotEqual(certificate&#91;"pairing_with_cubic_residual"&#93;, "0")
        self.assertEqual(
            result&#91;"universal_obstruction_for_all_finite_algebraic_ratios"&#93;,
            pairing_ideal&#91;"degree"&#93; == 0,
        )

        samples = result&#91;"sample_checks"&#93;
        self.assertEqual(len(samples), 9)
        for sample in samples:
            self.assertFalse(sample&#91;"cubic_lift_solvable"&#93;)
            self.assertGreater(
                sample&#91;"augmented_rank"&#93;,
                sample&#91;"effect_rank"&#93;,
            )
        if result&#91;"universal_obstruction_for_all_finite_algebraic_ratios"&#93;:
            self.assertEqual(certificate&#91;"pairing_degree"&#93;, 0)
            for sample in samples:
                self.assertNotEqual(sample&#91;"combined_pairing"&#93;, 0)

        print(
            json.dumps(
                {
                    "compressed_cubic_equation_count": result&#91;
                        "compressed_cubic_equation_count"
                    &#93;,
                    "effect_rank": result&#91;"generic_effect_rank_over_Q(r)"&#93;,
                    "augmented_rank": result&#91;
                        "generic_augmented_rank_over_Q(r)"
                    &#93;,
                    "left_kernel_dimension": result&#91;
                        "left_kernel_dimension_over_Q(r)"
                    &#93;,
                    "basis_pairings": &#91;
                        witness&#91;"pairing_with_cubic_residual"&#93;
                        for witness in basis
                    &#93;,
                    "pairing_ideal_generator": pairing_ideal&#91;"generator"&#93;,
                    "pairing_ideal_degree": pairing_ideal&#91;"degree"&#93;,
                    "combined_witness_nonzero_count": certificate&#91;
                        "nonzero_count"
                    &#93;,
                    "combined_witness_sha256": certificate&#91;"sha256"&#93;,
                    "combined_pairing": certificate&#91;
                        "pairing_with_cubic_residual"
                    &#93;,
                    "universal": result&#91;
                        "universal_obstruction_for_all_finite_algebraic_ratios"
                    &#93;,
                },
                indent=2,
                sort_keys=True,
            )
        )


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()
</code></pre>

<pre><code class="language-python">
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import sys
import unittest
from pathlib import Path

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent

sys.path.insert(0, str(RESEARCH_TOOLS))

from filtered_operation_complex.adapters.program5_rank_six_infinity_fibre_formal_scan import (  # noqa: E402
    analyze_infinity_fibre_formal_scan,
)


class Program5InfinityFibreFormalScanTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_coordinate_samples_are_lifted_through_order_four(self) -&gt; None:
        result = analyze_infinity_fibre_formal_scan(max_order=4)

        self.assertEqual(result&#91;"schema_version"&#93;, 1)
        self.assertEqual(
            result&#91;"source_sha256"&#93;,
            "a2ec1fb45cc42b527958f3c881b8a9bb8c0ce093aa553c846806ab868513a2d8",
        )
        self.assertEqual(result&#91;"infinity_fibre_dimension"&#93;, 18)
        self.assertEqual(result&#91;"sample_count"&#93;, 19)
        self.assertEqual(len(result&#91;"samples"&#93;), 19)

        by_name = {item&#91;"name"&#93;: item for item in result&#91;"samples"&#93;}
        base = by_name&#91;"infinity:base"&#93;
        self.assertEqual(base&#91;"first_obstructed_order"&#93;, 4)
        self.assertEqual(base&#91;"maximum_compatible_order"&#93;, 3)
        self.assertEqual(base&#91;"direct_effect_columns_verified"&#93;, 22)
        self.assertEqual(
            base&#91;"obstruction_certificate"&#93;&#91;"pairing_with_rhs"&#93;,
            "1/2",
        )

        xi5 = by_name&#91;"infinity:+xi_5"&#93;
        self.assertEqual(xi5&#91;"first_obstructed_order"&#93;, 3)
        self.assertEqual(xi5&#91;"maximum_compatible_order"&#93;, 2)

        for sample in result&#91;"samples"&#93;:
            obstruction = sample&#91;"first_obstructed_order"&#93;
            if obstruction is None:
                self.assertEqual(sample&#91;"maximum_compatible_order"&#93;, 4)
            else:
                self.assertEqual(
                    sample&#91;"maximum_compatible_order"&#93;,
                    obstruction - 1,
                )
                self.assertNotEqual(
                    sample&#91;"obstruction_certificate"&#93;&#91;"pairing_with_rhs"&#93;,
                    0,
                )

        print(
            json.dumps(
                {
                    "first_obstruction_histogram": result&#91;
                        "first_obstruction_histogram"
                    &#93;,
                    "survivor_count": result&#91;"survivor_count"&#93;,
                    "survivors": result&#91;
                        "survivors_through_requested_order"
                    &#93;,
                    "profiles": &#91;
                        {
                            "name": sample&#91;"name"&#93;,
                            "maximum_compatible_order": sample&#91;
                                "maximum_compatible_order"
                            &#93;,
                            "first_obstructed_order": sample&#91;
                                "first_obstructed_order"
                            &#93;,
                            "fixed_effect_rank": sample&#91;
                                "fixed_tangent_effect_rank"
                            &#93;,
                            "pairing": sample.get(
                                "obstruction_certificate", {}
                            ).get("pairing_with_rhs"),
                        }
                        for sample in result&#91;"samples"&#93;
                    &#93;,
                },
                indent=2,
                sort_keys=True,
            )
        )


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()

<pre><code class="language-python">
from __future__ import annotations

import json

import unittest
from pathlib import Path

RESEARCH_TOOLS = HERE.parent.parent

from filtered_operation_complex.adapters.program5_rank_six_line_formal_arc import (  # noqa: E402
    analyze_line_formal_arc,
)


class Program5RankSixLineFormalArcTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_theta_u_is_lifted_until_an_exact_obstruction_or_order_eight(self) -&gt; None:
        result = analyze_line_formal_arc(
            ratio=None,
            max_order=8,
            verify_effect=True,
        )

        self.assertEqual(result&#91;"schema_version"&#93;, 1)
        self.assertEqual(
            result&#91;"source_sha256"&#93;,
            "a2ec1fb45cc42b527958f3c881b8a9bb8c0ce093aa553c846806ab868513a2d8",
        )
        self.assertEqual(result&#91;"direction"&#93;, "theta_u")
        self.assertEqual(result&#91;"u_over_v"&#93;, "infinity")
        self.assertEqual(result&#91;"rank_six_tangent_dimension"&#93;, 22)
        self.assertEqual(result&#91;"direct_effect_columns_verified"&#93;, 22)
        self.assertGreaterEqual(result&#91;"maximum_compatible_order"&#93;, 3)

        first_obstructed = result&#91;"first_obstructed_order"&#93;
        if first_obstructed is None:
            self.assertEqual(result&#91;"maximum_compatible_order"&#93;, 8)
        else:
            self.assertGreaterEqual(first_obstructed, 4)
            self.assertEqual(
                result&#91;"maximum_compatible_order"&#93;,
                first_obstructed - 1,
            )
            certificate = result&#91;"obstruction_certificate"&#93;
            self.assertEqual(certificate&#91;"order"&#93;, first_obstructed)
            self.assertNotEqual(certificate&#91;"pairing_with_rhs"&#93;, 0)
            self.assertGreater(certificate&#91;"nonzero_count"&#93;, 0)

        print(
            json.dumps(
                {
                    "fixed_tangent_effect_rank": result&#91;
                        "fixed_tangent_effect_rank"
                    &#93;,
                    "maximum_compatible_order": result&#91;
                        "maximum_compatible_order"
                    &#93;,
                    "first_obstructed_order": first_obstructed,
                    "order_data": &#91;
                        {
                            "order": item&#91;"order"&#93;,
                            "compatible": item&#91;"compatible"&#93;,
                            "effect_rank": item.get("effect_rank"),
                            "augmented_rank": item.get("augmented_rank"),
                            "tangent_adjustment_count": item.get(
                                "tangent_adjustment", {}
                            ).get("coefficient_count"),
                            "correction_nonzero_count": item.get(
                                "finalized_correction", {}
                            ).get("nonzero_count"),
                            "pairing": item.get(
                                "obstruction_certificate", {}
                            ).get("pairing_with_rhs"),
                        }
                        for item in result&#91;"orders"&#93;
                    &#93;,
                },
                indent=2,
                sort_keys=True,
            )
        )


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()

## `research-tools/filtered_operation_complex/symbolic_tests/test_program5_rank_six_line_scan.py`

<pre><code class="language-python">
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import sys
import unittest
from pathlib import Path

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
RESEARCH_TOOLS = HERE.parent.parent
sys.path.insert(0, str(RESEARCH_TOOLS))

from filtered_operation_complex.adapters.program5_rank_six_line_scan import (  # noqa: E402
    analyze_line_scan,
)


class Program5RankSixLineScanTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_selected_projective_lines_are_audited_exactly(self) -&gt; None:
        ratios = (-2, -1, 0, 1, 2, 3, 4, 5)
        result = analyze_line_scan(ratios=ratios, verify_axes=True)

        self.assertEqual(result&#91;"schema_version"&#93;, 1)
        self.assertEqual(
            result&#91;"source_sha256"&#93;,
            "a2ec1fb45cc42b527958f3c881b8a9bb8c0ce093aa553c846806ab868513a2d8",
        )
        self.assertEqual(result&#91;"ambient_operation_dimension"&#93;, 115)
        self.assertEqual(result&#91;"rank_six_tangent_dimension"&#93;, 22)

        directions = result&#91;"finite_ratio_results"&#93; + &#91;result&#91;"infinity_result"&#93;&#93;
        self.assertEqual(len(directions), len(ratios) + 1)
        for direction in directions:
            self.assertTrue(direction&#91;"second_order_compatible"&#93;)
            self.assertEqual(direction&#91;"quadratic_tangent_freedom_dimension"&#93;, 22)
            self.assertEqual(
                direction&#91;"cubic_lift_solvable"&#93;,
                direction&#91;"cubic_effect_rank"&#93;
                == direction&#91;"cubic_augmented_rank"&#93;,
            )
            if direction&#91;"cubic_lift_solvable"&#93;:
                self.assertEqual(direction&#91;"maximum_compatible_order"&#93;, 3)
                self.assertIsNotNone(
                    direction&#91;"cubic_lift_solution_free_dimension"&#93;
                )
            else:
                self.assertEqual(direction&#91;"first_obstructed_order"&#93;, 3)
                certificate = direction&#91;"cubic_obstruction_certificate"&#93;
                self.assertNotEqual(certificate&#91;"pairing_with_rhs"&#93;, 0)
                self.assertGreater(certificate&#91;"nonzero_count"&#93;, 0)

        ratio_zero = next(
            item
            for item in result&#91;"finite_ratio_results"&#93;
            if item&#91;"u_over_v"&#93; == 0
        )
        self.assertEqual(ratio_zero&#91;"direct_effect_columns_verified"&#93;, 22)
        self.assertEqual(
            result&#91;"infinity_result"&#93;&#91;"direct_effect_columns_verified"&#93;,
            22,
        )

        print(
            json.dumps(
                {
                    "finite": &#91;
                        {
                            "u_over_v": str(item&#91;"u_over_v"&#93;),
                            "rank": item&#91;"cubic_effect_rank"&#93;,
                            "augmented_rank": item&#91;"cubic_augmented_rank"&#93;,
                            "solvable": item&#91;"cubic_lift_solvable"&#93;,
                            "pairing": item.get(
                                "cubic_obstruction_certificate", {}
                            ).get("pairing_with_rhs"),
                            "witness_sha256": item.get(
                                "cubic_obstruction_certificate", {}
                            ).get("witness_sha256"),
                        }
                        for item in result&#91;"finite_ratio_results"&#93;
                    &#93;,
                    "infinity": {
                        "rank": result&#91;"infinity_result"&#93;&#91;"cubic_effect_rank"&#93;,
                        "augmented_rank": result&#91;"infinity_result"&#93;&#91;
                            "cubic_augmented_rank"
                        &#93;,
                        "solvable": result&#91;"infinity_result"&#93;&#91;
                            "cubic_lift_solvable"
                        &#93;,
                        "pairing": result&#91;"infinity_result"&#93;.get(
                            "cubic_obstruction_certificate", {}
                        ).get("pairing_with_rhs"),
                    },
                },
                indent=2,
                sort_keys=True,
            )
        )


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()
</code></pre>

        )
        self.assertEqual(result&#91;"ambient_operation_dimension"&#93;, 115)

        self.assertEqual(result&#91;"quadratic_tangent_freedom_dimension"&#93;, 66)

## `research-tools/filtered_operation_complex/tests/test_adapters.py`

<pre><code class="language-python">
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import sys
import unittest
from pathlib import Path

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
RESEARCH_TOOLS = HERE.parent.parent
sys.path.insert(0, str(RESEARCH_TOOLS))

from filtered_operation_complex.adapters.program5_row_killing import (  # noqa: E402
    analyze_packet,
)
from filtered_operation_complex.adapters.program6_legacy import (  # noqa: E402
    analyze_legacy_document,
)


class AdapterTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_program6_legacy_k4_shape(self) -&gt; None:
        legacy = {
            "schema_version": 1,
            "name": "legacy synthetic k4",
            "layers": &#91;
                {
                    "label": "layer 1",
                    "operator": &#91;&#91;0, 1&#93;&#93;,
                    "gauge_vectors": &#91;&#91;1, 0&#93;&#93;,
                    "rechart_vectors": &#91;&#93;,
                },
                {
                    "label": "layer 2",
                    "operator": &#91;&#91;0, 0, 1&#93;&#93;,
                    "gauge_vectors": &#91;&#91;1, 0, 0&#93;, &#91;0, 1, 0&#93;&#93;,
                    "rechart_vectors": &#91;&#93;,
                },
                {
                    "label": "layer 3",
                    "operator": &#91;&#91;1, 0&#93;&#93;,
                    "gauge_vectors": &#91;&#91;0, 1&#93;&#93;,
                    "rechart_vectors": &#91;&#93;,
                },
                {
                    "label": "layer 4",
                    "operator": &#91;&#91;0, 1&#93;&#93;,
                    "gauge_vectors": &#91;&#93;,
                    "rechart_vectors": &#91;&#91;1, 0&#93;&#93;,
                    "support_transport": {"k": 4, "monomials": &#91;&#91;2, 2&#93;&#93;},
                },
            &#93;,
        }
        report = analyze_legacy_document(legacy)
        self.assertTrue(report&#91;"all_true_quotients_zero"&#93;)
        self.assertEqual(
            &#91;layer&#91;"kernel_dimension"&#93; for layer in report&#91;"layers"&#93;&#93;,
            &#91;1, 2, 1, 1&#93;,
        )
        self.assertEqual(report&#91;"layers"&#93;&#91;3&#93;&#91;"gauge_dimension"&#93;, 0)
        self.assertEqual(report&#91;"layers"&#93;&#91;3&#93;&#91;"rechart_increment"&#93;, 1)
        closure = report&#91;"adapter_metadata"&#93;&#91;"support_closures"&#93;&#91;0&#93;&#91;"closure"&#93;
        self.assertEqual(closure, &#91;&#91;2, 2&#93;, &#91;-2, 1&#93;, &#91;-6, 0&#93;&#93;)

    def test_program5_template(self) -&gt; None:
        packet = json.loads(
            (HERE.parent / "examples" / "program5_row_killing_template.json").read_text(
                encoding="utf-8"
            )
        )
        report = analyze_packet(packet)
        layer = report&#91;"layers"&#93;&#91;0&#93;
        self.assertEqual(layer&#91;"kernel_dimension"&#93;, 4)
        self.assertEqual(layer&#91;"gauge_dimension"&#93;, 3)
        self.assertEqual(layer&#91;"rechart_increment"&#93;, 1)
        self.assertEqual(layer&#91;"unexplained_dimension"&#93;, 0)
        self.assertEqual(layer&#91;"forcing_pairings"&#93;, &#91;"-1"&#93;)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()
</code></pre>

## `research-tools/filtered_operation_complex/tests/test_filtered_operation_complex.py`

<pre><code class="language-python">
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import sys
import unittest
from pathlib import Path

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
ROOT = HERE.parent.parent
EXAMPLE = HERE.parent / "examples" / "two_chart_rational.json"
sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT))

from filtered_operation_complex import ContractError, analyze_document  # noqa: E402


class FilteredOperationComplexTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def rational_contract(self) -&gt; dict:
        return json.loads(EXAMPLE.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))

    def test_rational_hierarchy_rechart_and_transport(self) -&gt; None:
        report = analyze_document(self.rational_contract())
        self.assertTrue(report&#91;"all_transitions_verified"&#93;)
        self.assertFalse(report&#91;"all_true_quotients_zero"&#93;)
        self.assertFalse(report&#91;"all_forcing_equations_solvable"&#93;)
        for layer in report&#91;"layers"&#93;:
            self.assertEqual(layer&#91;"kernel_dimension"&#93;, 3)
            self.assertEqual(layer&#91;"cokernel_dimension"&#93;, 1)
            self.assertEqual(layer&#91;"gauge_dimension"&#93;, 1)
            self.assertEqual(layer&#91;"rechart_increment"&#93;, 1)
            self.assertEqual(layer&#91;"unexplained_dimension"&#93;, 1)
            self.assertEqual(layer&#91;"forcing_pairings"&#93;, &#91;"1"&#93;)
            self.assertFalse(layer&#91;"forcing_solvable"&#93;)
            self.assertIsNone(layer&#91;"affine_solution_dimension"&#93;)
            self.assertEqual(
                layer&#91;"complete_left_null_forcing_pairings"&#93;,
                &#91;"1"&#93;,
            )

            actions = {entry&#91;"name"&#93;: entry for entry in layer&#91;"actions"&#93;}
            self.assertEqual(
                (
                    actions&#91;"formal"&#93;&#91;"source_dimension"&#93;,
                    actions&#91;"formal"&#93;&#91;"rank"&#93;,
                    actions&#91;"formal"&#93;&#91;"source_stabilizer_dimension"&#93;,
                ),
                (4, 3, 1),
            )
            self.assertEqual(
                (
                    actions&#91;"polynomial"&#93;&#91;"source_dimension"&#93;,
                    actions&#91;"polynomial"&#93;&#91;"rank"&#93;,
                    actions&#91;"polynomial"&#93;&#91;"source_stabilizer_dimension"&#93;,
                ),
                (3, 2, 1),
            )
            self.assertEqual(
                (
                    actions&#91;"filtered"&#93;&#91;"source_dimension"&#93;,
                    actions&#91;"filtered"&#93;&#91;"rank"&#93;,
                    actions&#91;"filtered"&#93;&#91;"source_stabilizer_dimension"&#93;,
                ),
                (2, 1, 1),
            )

        transition = report&#91;"transitions"&#93;&#91;0&#93;
        self.assertEqual(len(transition&#91;"operation_map_checks"&#93;), 3)
        self.assertTrue(
            all(
                item&#91;"action_square_verified"&#93;
                and item&#91;"source_stabilizer_transport_verified"&#93;
                for item in transition&#91;"operation_map_checks"&#93;
            )
        )
        self.assertEqual(
            transition&#91;"rechart_span_checks"&#93;&#91;0&#93;&#91;"transported_rank"&#93;,
            1,
        )
        quotient = transition&#91;"true_quotient_check"&#93;
        self.assertTrue(quotient&#91;"explained_space_transport_verified"&#93;)
        self.assertEqual(quotient&#91;"induced_quotient_rank"&#93;, 1)
        self.assertTrue(quotient&#91;"isomorphism_required"&#93;)
        self.assertTrue(
            transition&#91;"forcing_check"&#93;&#91;"dual_pairings_preserved"&#93;
        )
        self.assertEqual(transition&#91;"forcing_check"&#93;&#91;"pairings"&#93;, &#91;"1"&#93;)

    def test_solvable_affine_forcing_returns_particular_solution(self) -&gt; None:
        contract = {
            "schema_version": 1,
            "field": {"kind": "rational"},
            "report_options": {"include_vectors": True},
            "layers": &#91;
                {
                    "id": "solvable-affine-layer",
                    "deformation_dimension": 2,
                    "equation_dimension": 1,
                    "operator": &#91;&#91;1, 0&#93;&#93;,
                    "forcing": &#91;-2&#93;,
                }
            &#93;,
        }
        report = analyze_document(contract)
        layer = report&#91;"layers"&#93;&#91;0&#93;
        self.assertTrue(report&#91;"all_forcing_equations_solvable"&#93;)
        self.assertTrue(layer&#91;"forcing_solvable"&#93;)
        self.assertTrue(layer&#91;"forcing_compatibility_verified"&#93;)
        self.assertEqual(layer&#91;"affine_solution_dimension"&#93;, 1)
        self.assertEqual(layer&#91;"particular_solution"&#93;, &#91;"2", "0"&#93;)
        self.assertEqual(layer&#91;"complete_left_null_forcing_pairings"&#93;, &#91;&#93;)

    def test_generator_form_also_reports_source_stabilizer(self) -&gt; None:
        contract = {
            "schema_version": 1,
            "field": {"kind": "rational"},
            "layers": &#91;
                {
                    "id": "dependent-generators",
                    "deformation_dimension": 2,
                    "equation_dimension": 1,
                    "operator": &#91;&#91;1, 0&#93;&#93;,
                    "actions": &#91;
                        {
                            "name": "filtered",
                            "role": "filtered",
                            "generators": &#91;&#91;0, 1&#93;, &#91;0, 1&#93;&#93;,
                        }
                    &#93;,
                    "gauge_actions": &#91;"filtered"&#93;,
                }
            &#93;,
        }
        action = analyze_document(contract)&#91;"layers"&#93;&#91;0&#93;&#91;"actions"&#93;&#91;0&#93;
        self.assertEqual(action&#91;"input_form"&#93;, "generators")
        self.assertEqual(action&#91;"source_dimension"&#93;, 2)
        self.assertEqual(action&#91;"rank"&#93;, 1)
        self.assertEqual(action&#91;"source_stabilizer_dimension"&#93;, 1)

    def test_number_field_contract(self) -&gt; None:
        contract = {
            "schema_version": 1,
            "field": {
                "kind": "number_field",
                "modulus": &#91;-2, 0, 1&#93;,
                "symbol": "u",
            },
            "layers": &#91;
                {
                    "id": "quadratic-field",
                    "deformation_dimension": 2,
                    "equation_dimension": 1,
                    "operator": &#91;&#91;&#91;0, 1&#93;, &#91;1, 0&#93;&#93;&#93;,
                    "actions": &#91;
                        {
                            "name": "filtered",
                            "role": "filtered",
                            "generators": &#91;&#91;&#91;1, 0&#93;, &#91;0, -1&#93;&#93;&#93;,
                        }
                    &#93;,
                    "gauge_actions": &#91;"filtered"&#93;,
                }
            &#93;,
        }
        report = analyze_document(contract)
        layer = report&#91;"layers"&#93;&#91;0&#93;
        self.assertEqual(layer&#91;"kernel_dimension"&#93;, 1)
        self.assertEqual(layer&#91;"gauge_dimension"&#93;, 1)
        self.assertEqual(layer&#91;"unexplained_dimension"&#93;, 0)
        self.assertTrue(report&#91;"all_true_quotients_zero"&#93;)
        self.assertIsNone(report&#91;"all_forcing_equations_solvable"&#93;)
        self.assertEqual(
            layer&#91;"actions"&#93;&#91;0&#93;&#91;"source_stabilizer_dimension"&#93;,
            0,
        )

    def test_rejects_non_kernel_operation(self) -&gt; None:
        contract = self.rational_contract()
        contract&#91;"layers"&#93;&#91;0&#93;&#91;"actions"&#93;&#91;2&#93;&#91;"action_matrix"&#93;&#91;0&#93;&#91;0&#93; = 1
        with self.assertRaises(ContractError):
            analyze_document(contract)

    def test_rejects_false_parent_inclusion(self) -&gt; None:
        contract = self.rational_contract()
        contract&#91;"layers"&#93;&#91;0&#93;&#91;"actions"&#93;&#91;0&#93; = {
            "name": "formal",
            "role": "formal",
            "source_dimension": 1,
            "source_basis": &#91;"F0"&#93;,
            "action_matrix": &#91;&#91;0&#93;, &#91;1&#93;, &#91;0&#93;, &#91;0&#93;&#93;,
        }
        with self.assertRaises(ContractError):
            analyze_document(contract)

    def test_rejects_false_operation_transport_square(self) -&gt; None:
        contract = self.rational_contract()
        contract&#91;"transitions"&#93;&#91;0&#93;&#91;"operation_map_pairs"&#93;&#91;2&#93;&#91;"source_map"&#93; = &#91;
            &#91;0, 0&#93;,
            &#91;0, 0&#93;,
        &#93;
        with self.assertRaises(ContractError):
            analyze_document(contract)

    def test_action_requires_exactly_one_input_form(self) -&gt; None:
        contract = self.rational_contract()
        contract&#91;"layers"&#93;&#91;0&#93;&#91;"actions"&#93;&#91;0&#93;&#91;"generators"&#93; = &#91;&#91;0, 1, 0, 0&#93;&#93;
        with self.assertRaises(ContractError):
            analyze_document(contract)

    def test_json_roundtrip(self) -&gt; None:
        document = json.loads(json.dumps(self.rational_contract()))
        self.assertEqual(analyze_document(document)&#91;"schema_version"&#93;, 1)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()

## `research-tools/filtered_operation_complex/transition.py`

<pre><code class="language-python">
"""Exact chart-transition, operation-map, and dual-pairing checks."""
from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any, Mapping, Sequence

from .fields import ContractError, ExactField
from .layer import LayerAudit, RechartSpace
from .linear import (
    Matrix,
    Vector,
    dot,
    independent_extension,
    matrix_product,
    matrix_vector_product,
    parse_matrix,
    parse_vector,
    rank,
    rank_of_vectors,
    row_matrix_product,
    vector_in_span,
)


def _transport_vectors(
    vectors: Sequence&#91;Vector&#93;,
    matrix: Matrix,
    field: ExactField,
) -&gt; list&#91;Vector&#93;:
    return &#91;matrix_vector_product(matrix, vector, field) for vector in vectors&#93;


def _rechart_by_name(recharts: Sequence&#91;RechartSpace&#93;) -&gt; dict&#91;str, RechartSpace&#93;:
    return {rechart.name: rechart for rechart in recharts}


def audit_transition(
    field: ExactField,
    raw: Mapping&#91;str, Any&#93;,
    layers: Mapping&#91;str, LayerAudit&#93;,
) -&gt; dict&#91;str, Any&#93;:
    name = str(raw.get("name", "unnamed-transition"))
    from_id = str(raw.get("from"))
    to_id = str(raw.get("to"))
    if from_id not in layers or to_id not in layers:
        raise ContractError(f"{name}: transition references an unknown layer")
    source = layers&#91;from_id&#93;.data
    target = layers&#91;to_id&#93;.data
    deformation_map = parse_matrix(
        field,
        raw.get("deformation_map", &#91;&#93;),
        rows=target.deformation_dimension,
        columns=source.deformation_dimension,
        name=f"{name}.deformation_map",
    )
    equation_map = parse_matrix(
        field,
        raw.get("equation_map", &#91;&#93;),
        rows=target.equation_dimension,
        columns=source.equation_dimension,
        name=f"{name}.equation_map",
    )
    left = matrix_product(
        target.operator,
        deformation_map,
        left_rows=target.equation_dimension,
        middle=target.deformation_dimension,
        right_columns=source.deformation_dimension,
        field=field,
    )
    right = matrix_product(
        equation_map,
        source.operator,
        left_rows=target.equation_dimension,
        middle=source.equation_dimension,
        right_columns=source.deformation_dimension,
        field=field,
    )
    if left != right:
        raise ContractError(f"{name}: D_to*T_E != T_W*D_from")

    require_isomorphism = bool(raw.get("require_isomorphism", False))
    deformation_rank = rank(
        deformation_map,
        rows=target.deformation_dimension,
        columns=source.deformation_dimension,
        field=field,
    )
    equation_rank = rank(
        equation_map,
        rows=target.equation_dimension,
        columns=source.equation_dimension,
        field=field,
    )
    if require_isomorphism and (
        source.deformation_dimension != target.deformation_dimension
        or deformation_rank != source.deformation_dimension
        or source.equation_dimension != target.equation_dimension
        or equation_rank != source.equation_dimension
    ):
        raise ContractError(f"{name}: declared isomorphism has deficient rank")

    span_checks: list&#91;dict&#91;str, Any&#93;&#93; = &#91;&#93;
    raw_span_pairs = raw.get("operation_span_pairs", &#91;&#93;)
    if not isinstance(raw_span_pairs, list):
        raise ContractError(f"{name}: operation_span_pairs must be a list")
    for index, pair in enumerate(raw_span_pairs):
        if not isinstance(pair, Mapping):
            raise ContractError(
                f"{name}: operation span pair {index} must be an object"
            )
        from_action = str(pair.get("from"))
        to_action = str(pair.get("to"))
        if from_action not in source.actions or to_action not in target.actions:
            raise ContractError(
                f"{name}: operation span pair references an unknown action"
            )
        transported = _transport_vectors(
            source.actions&#91;from_action&#93;.basis,
            deformation_map,
            field,
        )
        for vector in transported:
            if not vector_in_span(
                vector,
                target.actions&#91;to_action&#93;.basis,
                ambient_dimension=target.deformation_dimension,
                field=field,
            ):
                raise ContractError(
                    f"{name}: transported {from_action} is not contained in "
                    f"{to_action}"
                )
        span_checks.append(
            {
                "from": from_action,
                "to": to_action,
                "transported_rank": rank_of_vectors(
                    transported,
                    ambient_dimension=target.deformation_dimension,
                    field=field,
                ),
                "inclusion_verified": True,
            }
        )

    operation_map_checks: list&#91;dict&#91;str, Any&#93;&#93; = &#91;&#93;
    raw_operation_maps = raw.get("operation_map_pairs", &#91;&#93;)
    if not isinstance(raw_operation_maps, list):
        raise ContractError(f"{name}: operation_map_pairs must be a list")
    for index, pair in enumerate(raw_operation_maps):
        if not isinstance(pair, Mapping):
            raise ContractError(
                f"{name}: operation map pair {index} must be an object"
            )
        from_action_name = str(pair.get("from"))
        to_action_name = str(pair.get("to"))
        if (
            from_action_name not in source.actions
            or to_action_name not in target.actions
        ):
            raise ContractError(
                f"{name}: operation map pair references an unknown action"
            )
        from_action = source.actions&#91;from_action_name&#93;
        to_action = target.actions&#91;to_action_name&#93;
        source_map = parse_matrix(
            field,
            pair.get("source_map", &#91;&#93;),
            rows=to_action.source_dimension,
            columns=from_action.source_dimension,
            name=f"{name}.operation_map_pairs&#91;{index}&#93;.source_map",
        )
        transported_action = matrix_product(
            deformation_map,
            from_action.action_matrix,
            left_rows=target.deformation_dimension,
            middle=source.deformation_dimension,
            right_columns=from_action.source_dimension,
            field=field,
        )
        target_action_after_source_map = matrix_product(
            to_action.action_matrix,
            source_map,
            left_rows=target.deformation_dimension,
            middle=to_action.source_dimension,
            right_columns=from_action.source_dimension,
            field=field,
        )
        if transported_action != target_action_after_source_map:
            raise ContractError(
                f"{name}: T_E*Theta_from != Theta_to*T_G "
                f"for {from_action_name}-&gt;{to_action_name}"
            )

        source_map_rank = rank(
            source_map,
            rows=to_action.source_dimension,
            columns=from_action.source_dimension,
            field=field,
        )
        require_source_isomorphism = bool(
            pair.get("require_isomorphism", False)
        )
        if require_source_isomorphism and (
            from_action.source_dimension != to_action.source_dimension
            or source_map_rank != from_action.source_dimension
        ):
            raise ContractError(
                f"{name}: declared operation-space isomorphism has deficient rank"
            )

        for stabilizer_vector in from_action.source_stabilizer_basis:
            transported_stabilizer = matrix_vector_product(
                source_map,
                stabilizer_vector,
                field,
            )
            if any(
                matrix_vector_product(
                    to_action.action_matrix,
                    transported_stabilizer,
                    field,
                )
            ):
                raise ContractError(
                    f"{name}: source stabilizer does not transport into "
                    f"the target stabilizer"
                )

        operation_map_checks.append(
            {
                "from": from_action_name,
                "to": to_action_name,
                "source_map_rank": source_map_rank,
                "source_dimensions": &#91;
                    from_action.source_dimension,
                    to_action.source_dimension,
                &#93;,
                "action_square_verified": True,
                "source_stabilizer_transport_verified": True,
                "isomorphism_required": require_source_isomorphism,
            }
        )

    source_recharts = _rechart_by_name(source.recharts)
    target_recharts = _rechart_by_name(target.recharts)
    rechart_span_checks: list&#91;dict&#91;str, Any&#93;&#93; = &#91;&#93;
    raw_rechart_pairs = raw.get("rechart_span_pairs", &#91;&#93;)
    if not isinstance(raw_rechart_pairs, list):
        raise ContractError(f"{name}: rechart_span_pairs must be a list")
    for index, pair in enumerate(raw_rechart_pairs):
        if not isinstance(pair, Mapping):
            raise ContractError(
                f"{name}: rechart span pair {index} must be an object"
            )
        from_rechart = str(pair.get("from"))
        to_rechart = str(pair.get("to"))
        if (
            from_rechart not in source_recharts
            or to_rechart not in target_recharts
        ):
            raise ContractError(
                f"{name}: rechart span pair references an unknown rechart"
            )
        transported = _transport_vectors(
            source_recharts&#91;from_rechart&#93;.basis,
            deformation_map,
            field,
        )
        for vector in transported:
            if not vector_in_span(
                vector,
                target_recharts&#91;to_rechart&#93;.basis,
                ambient_dimension=target.deformation_dimension,
                field=field,
            ):
                raise ContractError(
                    f"{name}: transported rechart {from_rechart} is not "
                    f"contained in {to_rechart}"
                )
        rechart_span_checks.append(
            {
                "from": from_rechart,
                "to": to_rechart,
                "transported_rank": rank_of_vectors(
                    transported,
                    ambient_dimension=target.deformation_dimension,
                    field=field,
                ),
                "inclusion_verified": True,
            }
        )

    true_quotient_check: dict&#91;str, Any&#93; | None = None
    if "true_quotient_map" in raw:
        quotient_options = raw&#91;"true_quotient_map"&#93;
        if not isinstance(quotient_options, Mapping):
            raise ContractError(f"{name}: true_quotient_map must be an object")
        transported_explained = _transport_vectors(
            source.explained_basis,
            deformation_map,
            field,
        )
        for vector in transported_explained:
            if not vector_in_span(
                vector,
                target.explained_basis,
                ambient_dimension=target.deformation_dimension,
                field=field,
            ):
                raise ContractError(
                    f"{name}: the true explained space does not transport"
                )

        transported_quotient = _transport_vectors(
            source.quotient_representatives,
            deformation_map,
            field,
        )
        for vector in transported_quotient:
            if any(matrix_vector_product(target.operator, vector, field)):
                raise ContractError(
                    f"{name}: quotient representative left the target kernel"
                )
        _, quotient_added = independent_extension(
            target.explained_basis,
            transported_quotient,
            ambient_dimension=target.deformation_dimension,
            field=field,
        )
        induced_rank = len(quotient_added)
        source_quotient_dimension = len(source.quotient_representatives)
        target_quotient_dimension = len(target.quotient_representatives)
        require_quotient_isomorphism = bool(
            quotient_options.get("require_isomorphism", False)
        )
        if require_quotient_isomorphism and (
            source_quotient_dimension != target_quotient_dimension
            or induced_rank != source_quotient_dimension
        ):
            raise ContractError(
                f"{name}: induced true-quotient map is not an isomorphism"
            )
        true_quotient_check = {
            "explained_space_transport_verified": True,
            "source_quotient_dimension": source_quotient_dimension,
            "target_quotient_dimension": target_quotient_dimension,
            "induced_quotient_rank": induced_rank,
            "isomorphism_required": require_quotient_isomorphism,
        }

    dual_checks: list&#91;dict&#91;str, Any&#93;&#93; = &#91;&#93;
    raw_dual_pairs = raw.get("dual_pairs", &#91;&#93;)
    if not isinstance(raw_dual_pairs, list):
        raise ContractError(f"{name}: dual_pairs must be a list")
    parsed_dual_pairs: list&#91;tuple&#91;Vector, Vector&#93;&#93; = &#91;&#93;
    for index, pair in enumerate(raw_dual_pairs):
        if not isinstance(pair, Mapping):
            raise ContractError(f"{name}: dual pair {index} must be an object")
        source_functional = parse_vector(
            field,
            pair.get("from", &#91;&#93;),
            width=source.equation_dimension,
            name=f"{name}.dual_pairs&#91;{index}&#93;.from",
        )
        target_functional = parse_vector(
            field,
            pair.get("to", &#91;&#93;),
            width=target.equation_dimension,
            name=f"{name}.dual_pairs&#91;{index}&#93;.to",
        )
        if (
            row_matrix_product(target_functional, equation_map, field)
            != source_functional
        ):
            raise ContractError(
                f"{name}: dual functional pullback does not match"
            )
        if any(
            row_matrix_product(source_functional, source.operator, field)
        ) or any(
            row_matrix_product(target_functional, target.operator, field)
        ):
            raise ContractError(
                f"{name}: supplied dual functional is not left-null"
            )
        parsed_dual_pairs.append((source_functional, target_functional))
        dual_checks.append({"index": index, "pullback_verified": True})

    forcing_check: dict&#91;str, Any&#93; | None = None
    if "forcing_pair" in raw:
        pair = raw&#91;"forcing_pair"&#93;
        if not isinstance(pair, Mapping):
            raise ContractError(f"{name}: forcing_pair must be an object")
        source_forcing = parse_vector(
            field,
            pair.get("from", &#91;&#93;),
            width=source.equation_dimension,
            name=f"{name}.forcing_pair.from",
        )
        target_forcing = parse_vector(
            field,
            pair.get("to", &#91;&#93;),
            width=target.equation_dimension,
            name=f"{name}.forcing_pair.to",
        )
        if (
            matrix_vector_product(equation_map, source_forcing, field)
            != target_forcing
        ):
            raise ContractError(f"{name}: forcing vector does not transport")
        pairings = &#91;&#93;
        for source_functional, target_functional in parsed_dual_pairs:
            source_pairing = dot(source_functional, source_forcing, field)
            target_pairing = dot(target_functional, target_forcing, field)
            if source_pairing != target_pairing:
                raise ContractError(
                    f"{name}: obstruction pairing is not preserved"
                )
            pairings.append(field.serialize(source_pairing))
        forcing_check = {
            "transport_verified": True,
            "dual_pairings_preserved": True,
            "pairings": pairings,
        }

    result: dict&#91;str, Any&#93; = {
        "name": name,
        "from": from_id,
        "to": to_id,
        "chain_map_verified": True,
        "deformation_map_rank": deformation_rank,
        "equation_map_rank": equation_rank,
        "isomorphism_required": require_isomorphism,
        "operation_span_checks": span_checks,
        "operation_map_checks": operation_map_checks,
        "rechart_span_checks": rechart_span_checks,
        "dual_checks": dual_checks,
    }
    if true_quotient_check is not None:
        result&#91;"true_quotient_check"&#93; = true_quotient_check
    if forcing_check is not None:
        result&#91;"forcing_check"&#93; = forcing_check
    return result
